================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================
# blrd ai

Коммерческий Telegram бот и веб-приложение для работы с множеством AI моделей через AnyModel.

**Функции:**
- Чат с выбором модели (DeepSeek, GPT, Claude, Gemini и др.)
- Веб-приложение (Mini App) с историей чатов
- Загрузка изображений и документов (PDF, DOCX, TXT)
- Подписка Pro через Platega
- Промокоды и рассылки
- Админ-панель: статистика, управление пользователями, конфиги моделей

**Стэк:** FastAPI, aiogram 3, SQLAlchemy, PostgreSQL, Telegram Bot API, AnyModel API

---

## Быстрый старт (локально, PyCharm)

### 1. Требования
- Python 3.11+
- PostgreSQL или SQLite (по умолчанию SQLite для разработки)

### 2. Клон и окружение
```bash
git clone https://github.com/YOUR_ORG/blrdai.git
cd blrdai
python -m venv .venv
.venv\Scripts\activate  # Windows
# или: source .venv/bin/activate  # Linux/macOS

pip install -r requirements.txt



================================================
FILE: deploy.sh
================================================
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# Конфиг
REPO="https://github.com/YOUR_ORG/blrdai.git"
BRANCH="${1:-main}"
USER="blrd"
HOME="/home/blrd"
APP="$HOME/app"
DOMAIN="${DOMAIN:-ai.example.com}"
EMAIL="${EMAIL:-admin@example.com}"

echo "=== blrd ai: развёртывание ==="
echo "Домен: $DOMAIN, ветка: $BRANCH"

# 1. Пользователь и папки
if ! id "$USER" &>/dev/null; then
  echo "→ Создаю пользователя $USER"
  useradd -m -s /bin/bash "$USER"
fi

sudo -u "$USER" mkdir -p "$APP" "$HOME/.env.d"
cd "$APP"

# 2. Клон репо
if [ ! -d .git ]; then
  echo "→ Клонирую $REPO"
  sudo -u "$USER" git clone --depth 1 -b "$BRANCH" "$REPO" .
else
  echo "→ Обновляю репо"
  sudo -u "$USER" git fetch origin "$BRANCH"
  sudo -u "$USER" git reset --hard "origin/$BRANCH"
fi

# 3. .env
ENV_FILE="$APP/.env"
if [ ! -f "$ENV_FILE" ]; then
  echo "→ Создаю .env (заполните вручную перед запуском)"
  cat > "$ENV_FILE" << 'EOF'
DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://blrd:change-me@localhost:5432/blrd
BOT_TOKEN=123456:ABCDEF...
BOT_USERNAME=blrd_ai_bot
WEBAPP_URL=https://DOMAIN.com
ANYMODEL_API_KEY=sk-...
PLATEGA_MERCHANT_ID=...
PLATEGA_SECRET=...
SECRET_KEY=change-me-to-64-random-chars
ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me
ADMIN_IDS=123456789
LOG_LEVEL=INFO
EOF
  chown "$USER:$USER" "$ENV_FILE"
  chmod 600 "$ENV_FILE"
  echo "   ⚠️  Отредактируйте: nano $ENV_FILE"
  echo "   Затем перезапустите: $0"
  exit 0
fi

# 4. PostgreSQL (если нет)
if ! command -v psql &> /dev/null; then
  echo "→ Устанавливаю PostgreSQL"
  apt-get update && apt-get install -y postgresql postgresql-contrib
  systemctl start postgresql
  systemctl enable postgresql
fi

if ! sudo -u postgres psql -lqt | cut -d \| -f 1 | grep -qw blrd; then
  echo "→ Создаю БД и пользователя"
  DB_PASS=$(grep DB_PASSWORD "$ENV_FILE" | cut -d= -f2)
  sudo -u postgres createuser blrd || true
  sudo -u postgres psql -c "ALTER USER blrd WITH PASSWORD '$DB_PASS';"
  sudo -u postgres createdb -O blrd blrd
fi

# 5. Python окружение
echo "→ Настраиваю Python"
sudo -u "$USER" python3 -m venv "$APP/venv" || true
sudo -u "$USER" "$APP/venv/bin/pip" install -q --upgrade pip
sudo -u "$USER" "$APP/venv/bin/pip" install -q -r "$APP/requirements.txt"

# 6. systemd для приложения
echo "→ Создаю systemd-сервис blrd-app"
cat > /etc/systemd/system/blrd-app.service << EOF
[Unit]
Description=blrd ai Application
After=network.target postgresql.service
Wants=postgresql.service

[Service]
Type=notify
User=$USER
WorkingDirectory=$APP
Environment="PATH=$APP/venv/bin"
EnvironmentFile=$ENV_FILE
ExecStart=$APP/venv/bin/python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8080
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
EOF

# 7. systemd для бота
echo "→ Создаю systemd-сервис blrd-bot"
cat > /etc/systemd/system/blrd-bot.service << EOF
[Unit]
Description=blrd ai Bot
After=network.target
Wants=blrd-app.service

[Service]
Type=simple
User=$USER
WorkingDirectory=$APP
Environment="PATH=$APP/venv/bin"
EnvironmentFile=$ENV_FILE
ExecStart=$APP/venv/bin/python run_bot.py
Restart=on-failure
RestartSec=10
StandardOutput=journal
StandardError=journal

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
EOF

systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable blrd-app blrd-bot

# 8. nginx
echo "→ Настраиваю nginx"
apt-get install -y nginx certbot python3-certbot-nginx

cat > /etc/nginx/sites-available/blrd << EOF
server {
    server_name $DOMAIN;
    listen 80;

    client_max_body_size 20M;

    location / {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
        proxy_set_header Host \$host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP \$remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For \$proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto \$scheme;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Connection "";
        proxy_buffering off;
    }

    # SSE (стрим генерации): не буферим
    location /api/chats/*/send {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8080;
        proxy_set_header Host \$host;
        proxy_buffering off;
        proxy_cache off;
        proxy_set_header X-Accel-Buffering no;
    }
}
EOF

ln -sf /etc/nginx/sites-available/blrd /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/ || true
rm -f /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default

if ! nginx -t &>/dev/null; then
  echo "   ⚠️  nginx конфиг ошибка, проверьте"
  nginx -t
  exit 1
fi

# 9. SSL (Certbot)
echo "→ Настраиваю SSL (Certbot)"
certbot --nginx -d "$DOMAIN" --email "$EMAIL" --agree-tos --no-eff-email --non-interactive || true

systemctl restart nginx

# 10. Старт сервисов
echo "→ Запускаю сервисы"
systemctl start blrd-app blrd-bot

echo ""
echo "=== ✅ Развёртывание готово ==="
echo "   Домен: https://$DOMAIN"
echo "   Бот: @$BOT_USERNAME"
echo "   Логи: journalctl -u blrd-app -f"
echo ""
echo "Чтобы остановить: systemctl stop blrd-app blrd-bot"
echo "Перезапуск конфига: systemctl restart blrd-app"

version: '3.9'

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:16-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: blrd
      POSTGRES_USER: blrd
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD:-blrd-dev}
    volumes:
      - pg_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "pg_isready", "-U", "blrd"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5
    networks:
      - app

  app:
    build: .
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql+asyncpg://blrd:${DB_PASSWORD:-blrd-dev}@db:5432/blrd
      BOT_TOKEN: ${BOT_TOKEN}
      BOT_USERNAME: ${BOT_USERNAME}
      WEBAPP_URL: ${WEBAPP_URL}
      ANYMODEL_API_KEY: ${ANYMODEL_API_KEY}
      PLATEGA_MERCHANT_ID: ${PLATEGA_MERCHANT_ID}
      PLATEGA_SECRET: ${PLATEGA_SECRET}
      SECRET_KEY: ${SECRET_KEY}
      ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${ADMIN_PASSWORD}
      ADMIN_IDS: ${ADMIN_IDS}
      LOG_LEVEL: ${LOG_LEVEL:-INFO}
    volumes:
      - ./data:/app/data
      - ./config:/app/config
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    networks:
      - app
    restart: unless-stopped

volumes:
  pg_data:

networks:
  app:

FILE: run_app.py

"""Локальный запуск API: Run ▶ в PyCharm или `python run_app.py`."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))


def preflight() -> None:
    """Проверяет, что app.main существует и отдаёт FastAPI, иначе объясняет что не так."""
    main_py = ROOT / "app" / "main.py"
    if (ROOT / "app" / "main").is_dir():
        raise SystemExit(f"{ROOT / 'app' / 'main'} — это папка, а должен быть файл main.py")
    if not main_py.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"нет файла {main_py}")
    if main_py.stat().st_size < 200:
        raise SystemExit(f"{main_py} почти пустой ({main_py.stat().st_size} байт) — файл не сохранён")

    import app.main as module

    target = getattr(module, "app", None)
    if target is None:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"в {module.__file__} нет объекта app. "
            "Должна быть строка: app = FastAPI(..., lifespan=lifespan)"
        )
    print(f"ok: {module.__file__} → {type(target).__name__}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    preflight()

    import uvicorn

    from app.core.config import settings

    print(f"API: http://127.0.0.1:{settings.port}  ·  health: /health")
    uvicorn.run(
        "app.main:app",
        host=settings.public_host,
        port=settings.port,
        reload=True,
        reload_dirs=[str(ROOT / "app"), str(ROOT / "web"), str(ROOT / "config")],
        log_level=settings.log_level.lower(),
    )

    main()

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, JSONResponse, ORJSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from app.api.routes import router as api_router
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import init_db
from app.services.anymodel import anymodel

logging.basicConfig(
    level=getattr(logging, settings.log_level.upper(), logging.INFO),
    format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s",
)
log = logging.getLogger("app")


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    await init_db()
    catalog.reload()
    log.info(
        "started: %d моделей, тарифы %s, 1 энергия = %d токенов",
        len(catalog.models()), list(catalog.plans()), catalog.energy_unit,
    )
    if not settings.anymodel_api_key:
        log.warning("ANYMODEL_API_KEY пуст — генерация ответов не заработает")
    yield
    await anymodel.close()


app = FastAPI(
    title="blrd ai",
    default_response_class=ORJSONResponse,
    docs_url=None,
    redoc_url=None,
    openapi_url=None,
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[settings.webapp_url, "http://localhost:8080", "http://127.0.0.1:8080"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

app.include_router(api_router)

try:  # админка появится в части 5; без неё приложение всё равно работает
    from app.admin.routes import router as admin_router

    app.include_router(admin_router)
except ImportError:
    log.warning("админ-панель не подключена")


@app.get("/health")
async def health() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {"ok": True, "models": len(catalog.models())}


@app.get("/pay/ok", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def pay_ok() -> str:
    return _pay_page("Оплата прошла", "Pro активирован. Можно закрывать страницу.", ok=True)


@app.get("/pay/fail", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def pay_fail() -> str:
    return _pay_page("Оплата не прошла", "Деньги не списаны. Попробуйте ещё раз.", ok=False)


def _pay_page(title: str, text: str, *, ok: bool) -> str:
    color = "#3ddc84" if ok else "#ff5b5b"
    return f"""<!doctype html><html lang="ru"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>{title}</title>
<script src="https://telegram.org/js/telegram-web-app.js"></script>
<style>body{{background:#000;color:#f2f2f2;font-family:-apple-system,Inter,sans-serif;
display:grid;place-items:center;height:100vh;margin:0;text-align:center;padding:24px}}
h1{{font-size:26px;margin:0 0 10px;color:{color}}}p{{color:#8e8e93;margin:0 0 24px}}
button{{background:#f2f2f2;color:#000;border:0;border-radius:16px;padding:15px 28px;
font-size:16px;font-weight:600}}</style></head>
<body><div><h1>{title}</h1><p>{text}</p>
<button onclick="window.Telegram?.WebApp?.close()||window.close()">Вернуться</button></div>
<script>window.Telegram?.WebApp?.ready();</script></body></html>"""


@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def unhandled(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
    log.exception("unhandled %s %s", request.method, request.url.path)
    return JSONResponse({"detail": "Внутренняя ошибка сервера"}, status_code=500)


# Mini App отдаётся последним: маршрут "/" не должен перехватывать /api
if settings.web_dir.exists():
    app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=str(settings.web_dir), html=True), name="web")
else:
    log.warning("папки %s нет — веб-приложение не отдаётся", settings.web_dir)

    @app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
    async def placeholder() -> str:
        return "<h1>blrd ai</h1><p>Создайте папку web/ с файлами Mini App.</p>"

================================================
FILE: app/admin/broadcast.py

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import datetime as dt
import logging
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import session_scope
from app.core.models import Broadcast, User, utcnow

log = logging.getLogger("broadcast")

SEND_DELAY = 0.06   # ~16 сообщений в секунду, лимит Telegram — 30
POLL_DELAY = 5.0
CAPTION_LIMIT = 1024


def photos_dir() -> Path:
    p = settings.data_dir / "broadcasts"
    p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return p


async def audience_ids(session: AsyncSession, audience: str) -> list[int]:
    now = utcnow()
    q = select(User.id).where(User.is_banned == False)  # noqa: E712
    if audience == "pro":
        q = q.where(User.plan == "pro", (User.plan_until == None) | (User.plan_until > now))  # noqa: E711
    elif audience == "free":
        q = q.where((User.plan != "pro") | ((User.plan_until != None) & (User.plan_until <= now)))  # noqa: E711
    elif audience == "active":
        q = q.where(User.last_seen >= now - dt.timedelta(days=7))
    rows = await session.execute(q)
    return [int(x) for x in rows.scalars()]


async def enqueue(
    session: AsyncSession, *, text: str, audience: str, photo_path: str | None
) -> Broadcast:
    ids = await audience_ids(session, audience)
    item = Broadcast(
        text=text, photo_path=photo_path, audience=audience,
        status="queued", total=len(ids),
    )
    session.add(item)
    await session.flush()
    return item


async def worker(bot) -> None:
    """Фоновая задача в процессе бота: берёт очередь и рассылает."""
    from aiogram.exceptions import (
        TelegramForbiddenError, TelegramRetryAfter, TelegramBadRequest,
    )
    from aiogram.types import FSInputFile

    log.info("рассылки: воркер запущен")
    while True:
        try:
            async with session_scope() as s:
                row = await s.execute(
                    select(Broadcast).where(Broadcast.status == "queued")
                    .order_by(Broadcast.id).limit(1)
                )
                item = row.scalar_one_or_none()
                if item is None:
                    bid = None
                else:
                    item.status = "running"
                    bid = item.id
                    text, photo, audience = item.text, item.photo_path, item.audience
                    ids = await audience_ids(s, audience)
                    item.total = len(ids)

            if bid is None:
                await asyncio.sleep(POLL_DELAY)
                continue

            log.info("рассылка %s: %d получателей", bid, len(ids))
            sent = failed = 0
            photo_file = None
            if photo:
                path = settings.data_dir / photo
                photo_file = FSInputFile(path) if path.exists() else None

            for i, uid in enumerate(ids, start=1):
                try:
                    if photo_file is not None:
                        await bot.send_photo(
                            uid, photo_file, caption=text[:CAPTION_LIMIT] or None
                        )
                    else:
                        await bot.send_message(uid, text[:4096], disable_web_page_preview=True)
                    sent += 1
                except TelegramRetryAfter as e:
                    log.warning("flood wait %ss", e.retry_after)
                    await asyncio.sleep(e.retry_after + 1)
                    continue
                except TelegramForbiddenError:
                    failed += 1  # бот заблокирован пользователем
                except TelegramBadRequest as e:
                    failed += 1
                    log.debug("send to %s failed: %s", uid, e)
                except Exception as e:
                    failed += 1
                    log.warning("send to %s crashed: %s", uid, e)

                await asyncio.sleep(SEND_DELAY)

                if i % 50 == 0:
                    async with session_scope() as s:
                        b = await s.get(Broadcast, bid)
                        if b:
                            b.sent, b.failed = sent, failed

            async with session_scope() as s:
                b = await s.get(Broadcast, bid)
                if b:
                    b.sent, b.failed = sent, failed
                    b.status = "done"
                    b.finished_at = utcnow()
            log.info("рассылка %s готова: %d отправлено, %d ошибок", bid, sent, failed)

        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            log.info("рассылки: воркер остановлен")
            return
        except Exception:
            log.exception("воркер рассылок упал, перезапуск через 15 с")
            await asyncio.sleep(15)

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import hmac
import logging
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request, UploadFile, File
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse
from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
from sqlalchemy import delete, desc, func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.admin import broadcast as bc
from app.admin import stats as stats_svc
from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core import settings_store
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import get_session
from app.core.models import Broadcast, Chat, Message, Payment, Promo, PromoUse, User, utcnow
from app.core.security import check_admin_cookie, issue_admin_session, require_admin
from app.services.notify import send_message

log = logging.getLogger("admin")
router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin")
templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(Path(__file__).parent / "templates"))
templates.env.filters["dtfmt"] = lambda v: v.strftime("%d.%m.%Y %H:%M") if v else "—"

PAGE_SIZE = 50


def _render(request: Request, name: str, ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> HTMLResponse:
    ctx.setdefault("ok", request.query_params.get("ok"))
    ctx.setdefault("err", request.query_params.get("err"))
    ctx["request"] = request
    ctx["weak_password"] = settings.admin_password in ("admin", "change-me", "")
    import inspect

    params = list(inspect.signature(templates.TemplateResponse).parameters)
    if params and params[0] == "request":
        return templates.TemplateResponse(request, name, ctx)
    return templates.TemplateResponse(name, ctx)



def _back(url: str, ok: str | None = None, err: str | None = None) -> RedirectResponse:
    sep = "&" if "?" in url else "?"
    if ok:
        url = f"{url}{sep}ok={ok}"
    elif err:
        url = f"{url}{sep}err={err}"
    return RedirectResponse(url, status_code=303)


# ── вход ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
@router.get("/login", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def login_page(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    if check_admin_cookie(request.cookies.get("badm")):
        return RedirectResponse("/admin", status_code=303)
    return _render(request, "login.html", {})


@router.post("/login")
async def login(request: Request, password: str = Form("")) -> RedirectResponse:
    if not settings.admin_password or not hmac.compare_digest(
        password, settings.admin_password
    ):
        log.warning("неудачный вход в админку с %s", request.client.host if request.client else "?")
        return _back("/admin/login", err="Неверный пароль")
    resp = RedirectResponse("/admin", status_code=303)
    resp.set_cookie(
        "badm", issue_admin_session("admin"),
        max_age=12 * 3600, httponly=True, samesite="lax",
        secure=settings.webapp_url.startswith("https://"),
    )
    return resp


@router.post("/logout")
async def logout() -> RedirectResponse:
    resp = RedirectResponse("/admin/login", status_code=303)
    resp.delete_cookie("badm")
    return resp


# ── дашборд ────────────────────────────────────────────────
@router.get("", response_class=HTMLResponse)
@router.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def dashboard(
    request: Request,
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> HTMLResponse:
    data = await stats_svc.dashboard(session)
    maint, maint_text = await settings_store.maintenance(session)

    users = data["users"]
    plan_donut = stats_svc.donut(
        [("Pro", users["pro"]), ("Free", max(0, users["total"] - users["pro"]))],
        ["#3ddc84", "#3a3a44"],
    )
    src = data["by_source"]
    source_donut = stats_svc.donut(
        [("Бот", src.get("bot", 0)), ("Веб", src.get("web", 0))],
        ["#5b9dff", "#9b6cff"],
    )
    top = data["by_model"][:6]
    peak = max((m["requests"] for m in top), default=1) or 1
    for m in top:
        m["width"] = round(m["requests"] / peak * 100)

    return _render(request, "dashboard.html", {
        "s": data, "maintenance": maint, "maintenance_text": maint_text,
        "plan_donut": plan_donut, "source_donut": source_donut, "top_models": top,
        "tab": "dash",
    })


# ── пользователи ───────────────────────────────────────────
@router.get("/users", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def users_page(
    request: Request,
    q: str = "",
    plan: str = "",
    page: int = 1,
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> HTMLResponse:
    page = max(1, page)
    query = select(User)
    if q.strip():
        term = f"%{q.strip().lower()}%"
        conds = [
            func.lower(func.coalesce(User.username, "")).like(term),
            func.lower(func.coalesce(User.first_name, "")).like(term),
            func.lower(func.coalesce(User.last_name, "")).like(term),
        ]
        if q.strip().isdigit():
            conds.append(User.id == int(q.strip()))
        query = query.where(or_(*conds))
    now = utcnow()
    if plan == "pro":
        query = query.where(User.plan == "pro", (User.plan_until == None) | (User.plan_until > now))  # noqa: E711
    elif plan == "free":
        query = query.where((User.plan != "pro") | ((User.plan_until != None) & (User.plan_until <= now)))  # noqa: E711
    elif plan == "banned":
        query = query.where(User.is_banned == True)  # noqa: E712

    total = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())
    ) or 0
    rows = await session.execute(
        query.order_by(desc(User.last_seen)).offset((page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE).limit(PAGE_SIZE)
    )
    users = list(rows.scalars())

    return _render(request, "users.html", {
        "users": users,
        "energy": {u.id: energy_svc.energy_display(u.energy_tokens) for u in users},
        "q": q, "plan": plan, "page": page,
        "pages": max(1, (total + PAGE_SIZE - 1) // PAGE_SIZE),
        "total": total, "tab": "users",
    })


@router.get("/users/{user_id}", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def user_page(
    user_id: int,
    request: Request,
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> HTMLResponse:
    data = await stats_svc.user_detail(session, user_id)
    if data is None:
        return _back("/admin/users", err="Пользователь не найден")
    data["tab"] = "users"
    data["models"] = catalog.models()
    return _render(request, "user.html", data)


@router.post("/users/{user_id}/action")
async def user_action(
    user_id: int,
    request: Request,
    action: str = Form(...),
    value: str = Form(""),
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    user = await session.get(User, user_id)
    if user is None:
        return _back("/admin/users", err="Пользователь не найден")
    url = f"/admin/users/{user_id}"

    if action == "ban":
        user.is_banned = True
        user.ban_reason = value.strip()[:512] or "Доступ закрыт администратором"
        await send_message(user_id, f"⛔️ <b>Доступ закрыт</b>\n{user.ban_reason}")
        return _back(url, ok="Пользователь заблокирован")

    if action == "unban":
        user.is_banned = False
        user.ban_reason = None
        await send_message(user_id, "✅ Доступ восстановлен.")
        return _back(url, ok="Блокировка снята")

    if action == "grant_pro":
        days = int(value) if value.strip().isdigit() else (catalog.pro().days or 30)
        energy_svc.grant_pro(user, days)
        await send_message(
            user_id,
            f"🎁 <b>Pro активирован</b> на {days} дней.\n"
            f"Начислено {catalog.pro().energy} энергии.",
        )
        return _back(url, ok=f"Pro выдан на {days} дней")

    if action == "revoke_pro":
        user.plan = "free"
        user.plan_until = None
        user.energy_tokens = energy_svc.plan_quota_base("free")
        user.refill_at = utcnow() + dt.timedelta(days=catalog.plan("free").refill_days or 30)
        return _back(url, ok="Pro снят")

    if action == "add_energy":
        try:
            amount = float(value.replace(",", "."))
        except ValueError:
            return _back(url, err="Введите число")
        energy_svc.add_energy(user, amount)
        if amount > 0:
            await send_message(user_id, f"⚡️ Начислено {amount:g} энергии.")
        return _back(url, ok=f"Энергия изменена на {amount:g}")

    if action == "reset_energy":
        user.energy_tokens = energy_svc.plan_quota_base("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")
        return _back(url, ok="Баланс сброшен к квоте тарифа")

    if action == "message":
        text = value.strip()
        if not text:
            return _back(url, err="Пустое сообщение")
        delivered = await send_message(user_id, text)
        return _back(url, ok="Сообщение отправлено") if delivered else _back(
            url, err="Telegram не принял сообщение (возможно, бот заблокирован)"
        )

    if action == "set_model":
        m = catalog.get(value)
        if m is None:
            return _back(url, err="Модель не найдена")
        user.current_model = m.path
        return _back(url, ok=f"Модель: {m.name}")

    if action == "make_admin":
        user.is_admin = not user.is_admin
        return _back(url, ok="Права администратора изменены")

    if action == "wipe_chats":
        chat_ids = (
            await session.execute(select(Chat.id).where(Chat.user_id == user_id))
        ).scalars().all()
        if chat_ids:
            await session.execute(delete(Message).where(Message.chat_id.in_(chat_ids)))
            await session.execute(delete(Chat).where(Chat.id.in_(chat_ids)))
        return _back(url, ok="Чаты удалены")

    if action == "delete":
        if value.strip() != str(user_id):
            return _back(url, err="Для удаления введите ID пользователя в поле подтверждения")
        chat_ids = (
            await session.execute(select(Chat.id).where(Chat.user_id == user_id))
        ).scalars().all()
        if chat_ids:
            await session.execute(delete(Message).where(Message.chat_id.in_(chat_ids)))
            await session.execute(delete(Chat).where(Chat.id.in_(chat_ids)))
        await session.execute(delete(PromoUse).where(PromoUse.user_id == user_id))
        await session.delete(user)
        log.warning("удалён пользователь %s", user_id)
        return _back("/admin/users", ok="Пользователь удалён")

    return _back(url, err="Неизвестное действие")

@router.post("/promos")
async def promo_create(
    code: str = Form(""),
    kind: str = Form("pro"),
    days: int = Form(30),
    energy: int = Form(0),
    max_uses: int = Form(1),
    expires_days: int = Form(0),
    comment: str = Form(""),
    count: int = Form(1),
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    count = max(1, min(count, 200))
    expires = utcnow() + dt.timedelta(days=expires_days) if expires_days > 0 else None
    created: list[str] = []

    for i in range(count):
        value = (code.strip().upper() if count == 1 and code.strip()
                 else f"{(code.strip().upper() or 'PRO')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}")
        exists = await session.scalar(select(Promo.id).where(Promo.code == value))
        if exists:
            if count == 1:
                return _back("/admin/promos", err="Такой промокод уже есть")
            continue
        session.add(Promo(
            code=value, kind=kind, days=max(1, days), energy=max(0, energy),
            max_uses=max(0, max_uses), expires_at=expires, comment=comment.strip()[:200] or None,
        ))
        created.append(value)

    await session.flush()
    return _back("/admin/promos", ok=f"Создано: {len(created)}")


@router.post("/promos/{promo_id}/toggle")
async def promo_toggle(
    promo_id: int,
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    promo = await session.get(Promo, promo_id)
    if promo is None:
        return _back("/admin/promos", err="Промокод не найден")
    promo.is_active = not promo.is_active
    return _back("/admin/promos", ok="Статус изменён")


@router.post("/promos/{promo_id}/delete")
async def promo_delete(

) -> RedirectResponse:

@router.post("/broadcasts")
async def broadcast_create(
    text: str = Form(""),
    audience: str = Form("all"),
    photo: UploadFile | None = File(None),
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    text = text.strip()
    rel_path: str | None = None

    if photo is not None and photo.filename:
        data = await photo.read()
        if len(data) > 10 * 1024 * 1024:
            return _back("/admin/broadcasts", err="Картинка больше 10 МБ")
        suffix = Path(photo.filename).suffix.lower() or ".jpg"
        if suffix not in (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"):
            return _back("/admin/broadcasts", err="Поддерживаются JPG, PNG, WEBP")
        name = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
        (bc.photos_dir() / name).write_bytes(data)
        rel_path = str(Path("broadcasts") / name)

    if not text and not rel_path:
        return _back("/admin/broadcasts", err="Добавьте текст или картинку")
    if rel_path and len(text) > bc.CAPTION_LIMIT:
        return _back("/admin/broadcasts",
                     err=f"С картинкой текст не длиннее {bc.CAPTION_LIMIT} символов")

    item = await bc.enqueue(session, text=text, audience=audience, photo_path=rel_path)
    return _back("/admin/broadcasts", ok=f"В очереди: {item.total} получателей")


@router.post("/broadcasts/{item_id}/cancel")
async def broadcast_cancel(
    item_id: int,
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    item = await session.get(Broadcast, item_id)
    if item is None:
        return _back("/admin/broadcasts", err="Рассылка не найдена")
    if item.status != "queued":
        return _back("/admin/broadcasts", err="Отменить можно только то, что ещё не началось")
    item.status = "failed"
    item.finished_at = utcnow()
    return _back("/admin/broadcasts", ok="Рассылка отменена")

    )
    return _render(request, "payments.html", {"rows": rows.all(), "tab": "payments"})


@router.post("/payments/{payment_id}/confirm")
async def payment_confirm(
    payment_id: int,
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    """Выдать Pro вручную, если колбэк потерялся, а деньги пришли."""
    payment = await session.get(Payment, payment_id)
    if payment is None:
        return _back("/admin/payments", err="Платёж не найден")
    if payment.applied:
        return _back("/admin/payments", err="Уже выдано")
    user = await session.get(User, payment.user_id)
    if user is None:
        return _back("/admin/payments", err="Пользователь не найден")
    energy_svc.grant_pro(user, payment.days)
    user.paid_total = (user.paid_total or 0) + int(payment.amount)
    payment.applied = True
    payment.status = "CONFIRMED"
    payment.paid_at = utcnow()
    await send_message(user.id, f"✅ <b>Pro активирован</b> на {payment.days} дней.")
    log.warning("платёж %s подтверждён вручную", payment_id)
    return _back("/admin/payments", ok="Pro выдан вручную")

@router.post("/settings")
async def settings_save(
    maintenance: str = Form(""),
    maintenance_message: str = Form(""),
    welcome_text: str = Form(""),
    per_minute: int = Form(20),
    support_url: str = Form(""),
    share_text: str = Form(""),
    bot_username: str = Form(""),
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    await settings_store.put(session, "maintenance", {
        "enabled": maintenance == "on",
        "message": maintenance_message.strip() or "Идут технические работы, скоро вернёмся.",
    })
    await settings_store.put(session, "welcome", {"text": welcome_text.strip()})
    await settings_store.put(session, "rate_limit", {"per_minute": max(1, min(per_minute, 300))})
    await settings_store.put(session, "links", {
        "support_url": support_url.strip(),
        "share_text": share_text.strip(),
        "bot_username": bot_username.strip().lstrip("@"),
    })
    return _back("/admin/settings", ok="Настройки сохранены")

@router.post("/config")
async def config_save(
    which: str = Form(...),
    text: str = Form(...),
    _: bool = Depends(require_admin),
) -> RedirectResponse:
    if which not in ("models", "plans"):
        return _back("/admin/config", err="Неизвестный файл")
    try:
        catalog.save_text(which, text)
    except Exception as e:
        return _back("/admin/config", err=f"YAML не сохранён: {e}")
    log.info("конфиг %s обновлён из админки", which)
    return _back("/admin/config", ok=f"{which}.yml сохранён, изменения применены")

FILE: app/admin/stats.py

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.models import Chat, Message, Payment, Promo, UsageLog, User, utcnow


async def dashboard(session: AsyncSession) -> dict[str, Any]:
    now = utcnow()
    day = now - dt.timedelta(days=1)
    week = now - dt.timedelta(days=7)

    users_total = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(User)) or 0
    users_day = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(User.created_at >= day)
    ) or 0
    users_week = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(User.created_at >= week)
    ) or 0
    active_week = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(User.last_seen >= week)
    ) or 0
    banned = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(User.is_banned == True)  # noqa: E712
    ) or 0
    pro_active = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(
            User.plan == "pro", (User.plan_until == None) | (User.plan_until > now)  # noqa: E711
        )
    ) or 0

    revenue_total = await session.scalar(
        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(Payment.amount), 0)).where(Payment.applied == True)  # noqa: E712
    ) or 0
    revenue_month = await session.scalar(
        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(Payment.amount), 0)).where(
            Payment.applied == True, Payment.paid_at >= now - dt.timedelta(days=30)  # noqa: E712
        )
    ) or 0
    payments_paid = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(Payment).where(Payment.applied == True)  # noqa: E712
    ) or 0
    payments_pending = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(Payment).where(Payment.status == "PENDING")
    ) or 0

    requests_total = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(UsageLog)) or 0
    requests_day = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(UsageLog).where(UsageLog.created_at >= day)
    ) or 0
    tokens_total = await session.scalar(
        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(UsageLog.base_cost), 0))
    ) or 0
    tokens_day = await session.scalar(
        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(UsageLog.base_cost), 0)).where(UsageLog.created_at >= day)
    ) or 0
    searches = await session.scalar(
        select(func.coalesce(func.sum(UsageLog.searches), 0))
    ) or 0

    chats = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Chat)) or 0
    messages = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Message)) or 0

    by_model = (
        await session.execute(
            select(
                UsageLog.model,
                func.count().label("n"),
                func.coalesce(func.sum(UsageLog.base_cost), 0).label("cost"),
            )
            .group_by(UsageLog.model)
            .order_by(func.count().desc())
            .limit(12)
        )
    ).all()

    by_source = (
        await session.execute(
            select(UsageLog.source, func.count()).group_by(UsageLog.source)
        )
    ).all()

    # дневной ряд считаем в Python: один SQL на оба движка
    rows = (
        await session.execute(
            select(UsageLog.created_at, UsageLog.base_cost).where(
                UsageLog.created_at >= now - dt.timedelta(days=14)
            )
        )
    ).all()
    buckets: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for i in range(13, -1, -1):
        key = (now - dt.timedelta(days=i)).strftime("%d.%m")
        buckets[key] = {"requests": 0, "tokens": 0}
    for created, cost in rows:
        key = created.strftime("%d.%m")
        if key in buckets:
            buckets[key]["requests"] += 1
            buckets[key]["tokens"] += int(cost or 0)
    peak = max((b["requests"] for b in buckets.values()), default=0) or 1
    series = [
        {"day": k, "requests": v["requests"], "tokens": v["tokens"],
         "height": round(v["requests"] / peak * 100)}
        for k, v in buckets.items()
    ]

    promos_active = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(Promo).where(Promo.is_active == True)  # noqa: E712
    ) or 0

    unit = catalog.energy_unit
    return {
        "users": {
            "total": users_total, "day": users_day, "week": users_week,
            "active_week": active_week, "pro": pro_active, "banned": banned,
            "conversion": round(pro_active / users_total * 100, 1) if users_total else 0.0,
        },
        "money": {
            "total": int(revenue_total), "month": int(revenue_month),
            "paid": payments_paid, "pending": payments_pending,
            "arpu": round(revenue_total / users_total, 1) if users_total else 0.0,
        },
        "usage": {
            "requests": requests_total, "requests_day": requests_day,
            "tokens": int(tokens_total), "tokens_day": int(tokens_day),
            "energy": energy_svc.energy_display(int(tokens_total)),
            "energy_day": energy_svc.energy_display(int(tokens_day)),
            "searches": int(searches),
            "cost_usd": round(int(tokens_total) / 1_000_000 * 0.05, 2),
        },
        "content": {"chats": chats, "messages": messages},
        "by_model": [
            {"model": m or "—", "name": (catalog.get(m).name if catalog.get(m) else m or "—"),
             "requests": n, "energy": energy_svc.energy_display(int(c))}
            for m, n, c in by_model
        ],
        "by_source": {s or "—": n for s, n in by_source},
        "series": series,
        "promos_active": promos_active,
        "unit": unit,
    }


async def user_detail(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    user = await session.get(User, user_id)
    if user is None:
        return None

    chats = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(Chat).where(Chat.user_id == user_id)
    ) or 0
    messages = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(Message).join(Chat).where(Chat.user_id == user_id)
    ) or 0
    logs = (
        await session.execute(
            select(UsageLog).where(UsageLog.user_id == user_id)
            .order_by(UsageLog.id.desc()).limit(25)
        )
    ).scalars().all()
    payments = (
        await session.execute(
            select(Payment).where(Payment.user_id == user_id)
            .order_by(Payment.id.desc()).limit(10)
        )
    ).scalars().all()
    recent_chats = (
        await session.execute(
            select(Chat).where(Chat.user_id == user_id)
            .order_by(Chat.updated_at.desc()).limit(10)
        )
    ).scalars().all()

    plan = catalog.plan("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")
    return {
        "user": user,
        "plan": plan,
        "energy": energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens),
        "spent_energy": energy_svc.energy_display(user.spent_tokens or 0),
        "chats": chats,
        "messages": messages,
        "logs": logs,
        "payments": payments,
        "recent_chats": recent_chats,
    }

def donut(parts: list[tuple[str, int]], colors: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Данные для SVG-пончика: доли, смещения, цвета."""
    total = sum(v for _, v in parts) or 1
    r = 54.0
    circ = 2 * 3.14159265 * r
    out, offset = [], 0.0
    for i, (name, value) in enumerate(parts):
        frac = value / total
        out.append({
            "name": name, "value": value,
            "pct": round(frac * 100, 1),
            "dash": round(circ * frac, 2),
            "gap": round(circ * (1 - frac), 2),
            "offset": round(-offset, 2),
            "color": colors[i % len(colors)],
        })
        offset += circ * frac
    return {"total": total, "circ": round(circ, 2), "r": r, "items": out}


async def chats_page(
    session: AsyncSession, *, user_id: int | None = None, page: int = 1, size: int = 40
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    q = select(Chat, User).join(User, User.id == Chat.user_id)
    if user_id is not None:
        q = q.where(Chat.user_id == user_id)

    total = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(q.subquery())) or 0
    rows = (
        await session.execute(
            q.order_by(Chat.updated_at.desc()).offset((page - 1) * size).limit(size)
        )
    ).all()

    ids = [c.id for c, _ in rows]
    counts: dict[int, int] = {}
    if ids:
        cnt = await session.execute(
            select(Message.chat_id, func.count())
            .where(Message.chat_id.in_(ids)).group_by(Message.chat_id)
        )
        counts = {cid: n for cid, n in cnt.all()}

    return {
        "rows": [{"chat": c, "user": u, "messages": counts.get(c.id, 0)} for c, u in rows],
        "total": total,
        "page": page,
        "pages": max(1, (total + size - 1) // size),
    }


async def chat_detail(session: AsyncSession, chat_id: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    chat = await session.get(Chat, chat_id)
    if chat is None:
        return None
    user = await session.get(User, chat.user_id)
    msgs = (
        await session.execute(
            select(Message).where(Message.chat_id == chat_id).order_by(Message.id)
        )
    ).scalars().all()
    spent = sum(int(m.base_cost or 0) for m in msgs)
    return {
        "chat": chat,
        "user": user,
        "messages": msgs,
        "spent_energy": energy_svc.energy_display(spent),
        "spent_tokens": spent,
    }

{% extends "base.html" %}

  <span class="nowrap">
    <a class="btn ghost sm" href="/admin/chats?user_id={{ chat.user_id }}">← Чаты пользователя</a>

          onsubmit="return confirm('Удалить чат вместе с перепиской?')">
      <button class="btn danger sm">Удалить чат</button></form>
  </span>
</div>

  <div class="kpi"><div class="v">{{ messages|length }}</div><div class="l">Сообщений</div></div>
  <div class="kpi"><div class="v">{{ spent_energy }}</div><div class="l">Энергии списано</div>
    <div class="s">{{ spent_tokens }} базовых токенов</div></div>
  <div class="kpi"><div class="v" style="font-size:19px">{{ 'бот' if chat.source == 'bot' else 'веб' }}</div>

    <div class="l">Пользователь</div>
    <div class="s">{{ chat.created_at|dtfmt }}</div></div>
</div>

  <div class="talk">
    {% for m in messages %}
      {% if m.role == 'user' %}
        <div class="bub u">
          {% if m.attachments %}
            <div class="atts">
              {% for a in m.attachments %}
                {% if a.kind == 'image' %}
                  <img class="ph" src="/api/files/{{ a.id }}" alt="" loading="lazy"
                       onerror="this.replaceWith(Object.assign(document.createElement('div'),{className:'att',textContent:'🖼 {{ a.name }}'}))">
                {% else %}
                  <span class="att">📄 {{ a.name }}</span>
                {% endif %}
              {% endfor %}
            </div>
          {% endif %}
          {{ m.content }}
          <div class="meta"><span>{{ m.created_at|dtfmt }}</span></div>
        </div>
      {% else %}
        <div class="bub a">
          {% if m.reasoning %}<div class="think">{{ m.reasoning }}</div>{% endif %}

{% block title %}Статистика{% endblock %}

{% macro donut(d, label, sub) %}
<svg class="donut" width="150" height="150" viewBox="0 0 150 150">
  <circle cx="75" cy="75" r="{{ d.r }}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.05)" stroke-width="15"/>
  {% for it in d['items'] %}
    {% if it.value %}
    <circle cx="75" cy="75" r="{{ d.r }}" fill="none" stroke="{{ it.color }}" stroke-width="15"
      stroke-dasharray="{{ it.dash }} {{ it.gap }}" stroke-dashoffset="{{ it.offset }}"
      stroke-linecap="round" transform="rotate(-90 75 75)"/>
    {% endif %}
  {% endfor %}
  <text x="75" y="72" text-anchor="middle" font-size="25">{{ label }}</text>
  <text x="75" y="92" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11.5" class="sub">{{ sub }}</text>
</svg>
<div class="legend">
  {% for it in d['items'] %}
  <div class="it"><span class="dot" style="background:{{ it.color }}"></span>
    <span class="nm">{{ it.name }}</span><span class="vl">{{ it.value }} · {{ it.pct }}%</span></div>
  {% endfor %}
</div>
{% endmacro %}

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any

import orjson
from fastapi import (
    APIRouter, BackgroundTasks, Depends, File, Form, HTTPException, Request,
    Response, UploadFile, status,
)
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, StreamingResponse
from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core import settings_store
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import get_session
from app.core.models import Attachment, Chat, Message, Payment, User, utcnow
from app.core.security import current_user, issue_session, upsert_user, verify_init_data
from app.services import files as files_svc
from app.services import pipeline, platega, promo as promo_svc
from app.services.limits import busy_users, limiter
from app.api.schemas import AuthIn, ChatIn, PromoIn, SendIn, user_public

log = logging.getLogger("api")
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api")


def _plan_of(user: User):
    return catalog.plan("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")


def _me(user: User) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return user_public(
        user,
        energy_display=energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens),
        plan=_plan_of(user),
    )


# ── авторизация ────────────────────────────────────────────
@router.post("/auth/telegram")
async def auth_telegram(
    payload: AuthIn,
    response: Response,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        data = verify_init_data(payload.init_data)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, f"Telegram не подтвердил вход: {e}") from e

    user = await upsert_user(session, data["user"], source="web")
    if user.is_banned:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, user.ban_reason or "Доступ заблокирован")

    token = issue_session(user.id)
    response.set_cookie(
        "bs", token, max_age=30 * 24 * 3600, httponly=True, samesite="none", secure=True
    )
    off, text = await settings_store.maintenance(session)
    return {
        "session": token,
        "user": _me(user),
        "maintenance": {"enabled": off, "message": text},
    }

@router.post("/auth/dev")
async def auth_dev(
    response: Response, session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Только для локальной отладки: включается DEV_AUTH_ID в .env."""
    if not settings.dev_auth_id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "not found")
    if settings.webapp_url.startswith("https://"):
        log.warning("DEV_AUTH_ID активен на публичном домене — выключите его")

    user = await upsert_user(
        session,
        {"id": settings.dev_auth_id, "first_name": "Local", "last_name": "Dev",
         "username": "localdev", "language_code": "ru"},
        source="web",
    )
    token = issue_session(user.id)
    response.set_cookie("bs", token, max_age=30 * 24 * 3600, httponly=True, samesite="lax")
    return {"session": token, "user": _me(user), "maintenance": {"enabled": False, "message": ""}}

@router.get("/me")
async def me(
    user: User = Depends(current_user), session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    off, text = await settings_store.maintenance(session)
    return {"user": _me(user), "maintenance": {"enabled": off, "message": text}}

@router.get("/config")
async def public_config(
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    data = await settings_store.links(session)
    data["app_name"] = "БЕЛЕБАЛАРДЫХ AI"
    return data

# ── модели и тарифы ────────────────────────────────────────
@router.get("/models")
async def models(user: User = Depends(current_user)) -> dict[str, Any]:
    plan = _plan_of(user)
    pro = catalog.pro()
    return {
        "models": [m.to_public(plan.code) for m in catalog.models()],
        "current": user.current_model or catalog.default_model(plan.code).path,
        "plan": plan.code,
        "energy": energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens),
        "energy_max": plan.energy,
        "pro": {
            "price_rub": pro.price_rub,
            "days": pro.days,
            "energy": pro.energy,
            "features": list(pro.features),
        },
    }


@router.post("/models/select")
async def select_model(
    body: ChatIn,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    m = catalog.get(body.model)
    if m is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Модель не найдена")
    if not m.allowed_for(_plan_of(user).code):
        raise HTTPException(403, "Модель доступна в Pro")
    user.current_model = m.path
    await session.flush()
    return {"ok": True, "model": m.path}


# ── чаты ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
@router.get("/chats")
async def list_chats(
    user: User = Depends(current_user), session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows = await session.execute(
        select(Chat)
        .where(Chat.user_id == user.id, Chat.is_deleted == False)  # noqa: E712
        .order_by(Chat.updated_at.desc())
        .limit(200)
    )
    chats = list(rows.scalars())
    return {
        "chats": [
            {
                "id": c.id,
                "title": c.title,
                "model": c.model,
                "updated_at": c.updated_at.isoformat(),
            }
            for c in chats
        ]
    }


@router.post("/chats")
async def create_chat(
    body: ChatIn,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    model = catalog.get(body.model) or catalog.get(user.current_model) or catalog.default_model(
        _plan_of(user).code
    )
    chat = Chat(user_id=user.id, title=body.title or "Новый чат", model=model.path, source="web")
    session.add(chat)
    await session.flush()
    return {"id": chat.id, "title": chat.title, "model": chat.model}


@router.get("/chats/{chat_id}")
async def get_chat(
    chat_id: int,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    chat = await session.get(Chat, chat_id)
    if chat is None or chat.user_id != user.id or chat.is_deleted:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Чат не найден")
    rows = await session.execute(
        select(Message).where(Message.chat_id == chat.id).order_by(Message.id)
    )
    msgs = list(rows.scalars())
    return {
        "id": chat.id,
        "title": chat.title,
        "model": chat.model,
        "messages": [
            {
                "id": m.id,
                "role": m.role,
                "content": m.content,
                "reasoning": m.reasoning,
                "atts": m.attachments or [],
                "created_at": m.created_at.isoformat(),
            }
            for m in msgs
        ],
    }


@router.patch("/chats/{chat_id}")
async def patch_chat(
    chat_id: int,
    body: ChatIn,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    chat = await session.get(Chat, chat_id)
    if chat is None or chat.user_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Чат не найден")
    if body.title is not None:
        chat.title = body.title[:200] or chat.title
    if body.model:
        m = catalog.get(body.model)
        if m and m.allowed_for(_plan_of(user).code):
            chat.model = m.path
            user.current_model = m.path
    chat.updated_at = utcnow()
    await session.flush()
    return {"ok": True}


@router.delete("/chats/{chat_id}")
async def delete_chat(
    chat_id: int,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    chat = await session.get(Chat, chat_id)
    if chat is None or chat.user_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Чат не найден")
    await session.execute(delete(Message).where(Message.chat_id == chat.id))
    await session.delete(chat)
    return {"ok": True}


# ── генерация (SSE) ────────────────────────────────────────
async def _guard_generation(
    request: Request, user: User, session: AsyncSession
) -> None:
    """Проверки, общие для первой генерации и перегенерации."""
    off, text = await settings_store.maintenance(session)
    if off and not user.is_admin:
        raise HTTPException(503, text or "Идут технические работы")

    rl = await settings_store.get(session, "rate_limit") or {}
    if not limiter.check(user.id, int(rl.get("per_minute", 20))):
        raise HTTPException(429, "Слишком часто. Подождите немного.", headers={
            "Retry-After": str(limiter.retry_after(user.id))
        })
    if user.id in busy_users:
        raise HTTPException(409, "Предыдущий ответ ещё генерируется")


def _sse(request: Request, uid: int, **kwargs: Any) -> StreamingResponse:
    async def events():
        busy_users.add(uid)
        try:
            gen = pipeline.run(user_id=uid, **kwargs)
            async for ev in gen:
                if await request.is_disconnected():
                    await gen.aclose()
                    break
                yield b"data: " + orjson.dumps(ev) + b"\n\n"
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            raise
        except Exception as e:
            log.exception("stream crashed uid=%s", uid)
            yield b"data: " + orjson.dumps(
                {"type": "error", "message": f"Внутренняя ошибка: {e}", "code": "internal"}
            ) + b"\n\n"
        finally:
            busy_users.discard(uid)
            yield b"data: [DONE]\n\n"

    return StreamingResponse(
        events(),
        media_type="text/event-stream",
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
            "Connection": "keep-alive",
        },
    )


@router.post("/chats/{chat_id}/send")
async def send(
    chat_id: int,
    body: SendIn,
    request: Request,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> StreamingResponse:
    await _guard_generation(request, user, session)
    if not body.text.strip() and not body.attachments:
        raise HTTPException(400, "Пустой запрос")

    atts = [a.model_dump() for a in body.attachments]
    if atts:
        ids = [a["id"] for a in atts]
        rows = await session.execute(
            select(Attachment).where(Attachment.id.in_(ids), Attachment.user_id == user.id)
        )
        owned = {a.id: a for a in rows.scalars()}
        if len(owned) != len(ids):
            raise HTTPException(400, "Вложение не найдено")
        atts = [
            {"id": a.id, "kind": a.kind, "name": a.file_name, "mime": a.mime}
            for a in owned.values()
        ]

    uid, model_path, text = user.id, body.model, body.text
    await session.commit()  # стрим живёт долго, соединение освобождаем

    return _sse(
        request, uid,
        chat_id=chat_id, text=text, attachments=atts,
        source="web", model_path=model_path,
    )


@router.post("/chats/{chat_id}/regenerate")
async def regenerate(
    chat_id: int,
    body: ChatIn,
    request: Request,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> StreamingResponse:
    """Удаляет последний ответ модели и генерирует его заново по тому же вопросу."""
    await _guard_generation(request, user, session)

    chat = await session.get(Chat, chat_id)
    if chat is None or chat.user_id != user.id or chat.is_deleted:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Чат не найден")

    uid, model_path = user.id, body.model
    await session.commit()

    return _sse(
        request, uid,
        chat_id=chat_id, source="web", model_path=model_path, regenerate=True,
    )


# ── вложения ───────────────────────────────────────────────
@router.post("/files")
async def upload(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    plan = _plan_of(user)
    if plan.max_attachments <= 0:
        raise HTTPException(403, "Вложения доступны в Pro")
    data = await file.read()
    try:
        att = await files_svc.save_upload(
            session,
            user_id=user.id,
            data=data,
            file_name=file.filename or "file",
            mime=file.content_type or "application/octet-stream",
        )
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(e)) from e
    return {
        "id": att.id,
        "kind": att.kind,
        "name": att.file_name,
        "mime": att.mime,
        "size": att.size,
        "url": f"/api/files/{att.id}",
    }


@router.get("/files/{file_id}")
async def get_file(
    file_id: str,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
):
    att = await session.get(Attachment, file_id)
    if att is None or (att.user_id != user.id and not user.is_admin):
        raise HTTPException(404, "Файл не найден")
    path = files_svc.abs_path(att)
    if not path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(404, "Файл удалён")
    return FileResponse(path, media_type=att.mime, filename=att.file_name)


# ── оплата и промокоды ─────────────────────────────────────
@router.post("/payments/create")
async def create_payment(
    user: User = Depends(current_user), session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        payment = await platega.create_payment(session, user, source="web")
    except platega.PlategaError as e:
        log.error("payment create failed uid=%s: %s", user.id, e)
        raise HTTPException(502, str(e)) from e
    return {"payment_id": payment.id, "url": payment.pay_url, "amount": payment.amount}


@router.get("/payments/{payment_id}")
async def payment_status(
    payment_id: int,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payment = await session.get(Payment, payment_id)
    if payment is None or payment.user_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Платёж не найден")

    # подстраховка на случай потерянного колбэка
    if payment.status == platega.PENDING and payment.tx_id:
        try:
            remote = await platega.fetch_status(payment.tx_id)
            await platega.apply_callback(
                session, {"id": payment.tx_id, "status": remote.get("status"), "payload": ""}
            )
            await session.refresh(payment)
            await session.refresh(user)
        except platega.PlategaError as e:
            log.info("poll failed %s: %s", payment.tx_id, e)

    return {"status": payment.status, "applied": payment.applied, "user": _me(user)}


@router.post("/payments/platega/callback")
async def platega_callback(
    request: Request, session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not platega.callback_authorized({k.lower(): v for k, v in request.headers.items()}):
        raise HTTPException(403, "bad secret")
    try:
        data = await request.json()
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(400, "bad json") from e

    payment, granted = await platega.apply_callback(session, data)
    if granted and payment:
        from app.services.notify import notify_payment  # ленивый импорт: бот не нужен вебу

        asyncio.create_task(notify_payment(payment.user_id, payment.days))
    return {"ok": True}


@router.post("/promo/redeem")
async def redeem_promo(
    body: PromoIn,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        message = await promo_svc.redeem(session, user, body.code)
    except promo_svc.PromoError as e:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(e)) from e
    return {"ok": True, "message": message, "user": _me(user)}


# ── статистика пользователя ────────────────────────────────
@router.get("/usage")
async def usage(
    user: User = Depends(current_user), session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    total = await session.scalar(
        select(func.count()).select_from(Message).join(Chat).where(Chat.user_id == user.id)
    )
    plan = _plan_of(user)
    return {
        "requests": user.requests,
        "messages": int(total or 0),
        "spent_energy": energy_svc.energy_display(user.spent_tokens or 0),
        "energy": energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens),
        "energy_max": plan.energy,
        "plan": plan.code,
        "plan_until": user.plan_until.isoformat() if user.plan_until else None,
    }

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class AuthIn(BaseModel):
    init_data: str = Field(default="", alias="initData")

    model_config = {"populate_by_name": True}


class AttachmentIn(BaseModel):
    id: str
    kind: str = "doc"
    name: str = ""
    mime: str = ""


class SendIn(BaseModel):
    text: str = ""
    model: str | None = None
    attachments: list[AttachmentIn] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ChatIn(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = None
    model: str | None = None


class PromoIn(BaseModel):
    code: str


def user_public(user, *, energy_display, plan) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": user.id,
        "name": user.display_name,
        "username": user.username,
        "photo_url": user.photo_url,
        "plan": "pro" if user.is_pro else "free",
        "plan_title": plan.title,
        "plan_until": user.plan_until.isoformat() if user.plan_until else None,
        "energy": energy_display,
        "energy_max": plan.energy,
        "max_attachments": plan.max_attachments,
        "is_admin": user.is_admin,
        "model": user.current_model,
    }

from __future__ import annotations

from aiogram.types import (
    InlineKeyboardButton, InlineKeyboardMarkup, KeyboardButton,
    ReplyKeyboardMarkup, WebAppInfo,
)

from app.core.catalog import ModelSpec, catalog
from app.core.config import settings


def main_menu() -> ReplyKeyboardMarkup:
    rows = [
        [KeyboardButton(text="🧠 Модель"), KeyboardButton(text="⚡ Баланс")],
        [KeyboardButton(text="🆕 Новый чат"), KeyboardButton(text="💎 Pro")],
        [KeyboardButton(text="🆘 Поддержка")],
    ]
    if settings.webapp_url.startswith("https://"):
        rows.insert(0, [KeyboardButton(
            text="💬 Открыть приложение",
            web_app=WebAppInfo(url=settings.webapp_url),
        )])
    return ReplyKeyboardMarkup(keyboard=rows, resize_keyboard=True, is_persistent=True)


def webapp_button(text: str = "Открыть приложение") -> InlineKeyboardMarkup | None:
    if not settings.webapp_url.startswith("https://"):
        return None
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[
        [InlineKeyboardButton(text=text, web_app=WebAppInfo(url=settings.webapp_url))]
    ])


def support_kb(url: str) -> InlineKeyboardMarkup | None:
    if not url:
        return None
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[
        [InlineKeyboardButton(text="🆘 Написать в поддержку", url=url)]
    ])


def share_kb(bot_username: str, text: str) -> InlineKeyboardMarkup | None:
    if not bot_username:
        return None
    link = f"https://t.me/{bot_username}"
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[
        [InlineKeyboardButton(
            text="📣 Поделиться ботом",
            switch_inline_query=f"{text} {link}",
        )]
    ])


def stop_kb() -> InlineKeyboardMarkup:
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=[
        [InlineKeyboardButton(text="⏹ Остановить", callback_data="stop")]
    ])


def models_kb(plan: str, current: str | None) -> InlineKeyboardMarkup:
    rows: list[list[InlineKeyboardButton]] = []
    for i, m in enumerate(catalog.models()):
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(text=_model_label(m, plan, current), callback_data=f"m:{i}")])
    if plan != "pro":
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(text="💎 Открыть все модели — Pro", callback_data="pro")])
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=rows)


def _model_label(m: ModelSpec, plan: str, current: str | None) -> str:
    mark = "✅ " if m.path == current else ""
    if not m.allowed_for(plan):
        return f"🔒 {m.name} · PRO"
    price = "бесплатно" if m.free else f"×{m.multiplier:g}"
    eye = " 👁" if m.vision else ""
    return f"{mark}{m.name} · {price}{eye}"


def pro_kb(pay_url: str | None = None, payment_id: int | None = None) -> InlineKeyboardMarkup:
    rows: list[list[InlineKeyboardButton]] = []
    if pay_url:
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(text="💳 Перейти к оплате", url=pay_url)])
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(text="🔄 Я оплатил, проверить", callback_data=f"chk:{payment_id}")])
    else:
        pro = catalog.pro()
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(
            text=f"💎 Оформить Pro — {pro.price_rub} ₽", callback_data="pay")])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(text="🎁 У меня есть промокод", callback_data="promo")])
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(inline_keyboard=rows)

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging

from aiogram import Bot, Dispatcher
from aiogram.client.default import DefaultBotProperties
from aiogram.enums import ParseMode
from aiogram.fsm.storage.memory import MemoryStorage
from aiogram.types import BotCommand, MenuButtonCommands, MenuButtonWebApp, WebAppInfo

from app.bot.handlers import setup_routers
from app.bot.middlewares import UserMiddleware
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import init_db
from app.services.anymodel import anymodel

logging.basicConfig(
    level=getattr(logging, settings.log_level.upper(), logging.INFO),
    format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s",
)
log = logging.getLogger("bot")

COMMANDS = [
    BotCommand(command="new", description="Новый чат"),
    BotCommand(command="model", description="Выбрать нейросеть"),
    BotCommand(command="balance", description="Энергия и тариф"),
    BotCommand(command="pro", description="Подписка Pro"),
    BotCommand(command="promo", description="Ввести промокод"),
    BotCommand(command="support", description="Поддержка"),
    BotCommand(command="share", description="Поделиться ботом"),
    BotCommand(command="stop", description="Прервать ответ"),
    BotCommand(command="help", description="Что умеет бот"),
]


def build() -> tuple[Bot, Dispatcher]:
    if not settings.bot_token:
        raise SystemExit("BOT_TOKEN не задан в .env")

    bot = Bot(
        settings.bot_token,
        default=DefaultBotProperties(parse_mode=ParseMode.HTML, link_preview_is_disabled=True),
    )
    dp = Dispatcher(storage=MemoryStorage())
    dp.update.middleware(UserMiddleware())
    dp.include_router(setup_routers())
    return bot, dp


async def run() -> None:
    await init_db()
    catalog.reload()
    bot, dp = build()

    me = await bot.get_me()
    log.info("бот @%s запущен, моделей: %d", me.username, len(catalog.models()))

    await bot.set_my_commands(COMMANDS)
    if settings.webapp_url.startswith("https://"):
        await bot.set_chat_menu_button(
            menu_button=MenuButtonWebApp(text="Приложение", web_app=WebAppInfo(url=settings.webapp_url))
        )
    else:
        await bot.set_chat_menu_button(menu_button=MenuButtonCommands())
        log.warning("WEBAPP_URL не https — кнопка Mini App отключена")

    try:
        from app.admin.broadcast import worker  # появится в части 5

        asyncio.create_task(worker(bot))
    except ImportError:
        pass

    try:
        await bot.delete_webhook(drop_pending_updates=False)
        await dp.start_polling(bot, allowed_updates=dp.resolve_used_update_types())
    finally:
        await anymodel.close()
        await bot.session.close()


def main() -> None:
    try:
        asyncio.run(run())
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit):
        log.info("бот остановлен")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: app/bot/middlewares.py

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable

from aiogram import BaseMiddleware
from aiogram.types import CallbackQuery, Message, TelegramObject, User as TgUser

from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core import settings_store
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import session_scope
from app.core.security import upsert_user

log = logging.getLogger("bot.mw")


class UserMiddleware(BaseMiddleware):
    """Создаёт/обновляет пользователя и кладёт его в data['user']."""

    async def __call__(
        self,
        handler: Callable[[TelegramObject, dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[Any]],
        event: TelegramObject,
        data: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> Any:
        tg: TgUser | None = data.get("event_from_user")
        if tg is None or tg.is_bot:
            return None

        async with session_scope() as s:
            user = await upsert_user(
                s,
                {
                    "id": tg.id,
                    "username": tg.username,
                    "first_name": tg.first_name,
                    "last_name": tg.last_name,
                    "language_code": tg.language_code,
                },
                source="bot",
            )
            maintenance, maint_text = await settings_store.maintenance(s)
            energy_svc.ensure_plan_state(user)

        if user.is_banned:
            text = user.ban_reason or "Доступ к боту закрыт."
            if isinstance(event, Message):
                await event.answer(f"⛔️ {text}")
            elif isinstance(event, CallbackQuery):
                await event.answer(text, show_alert=True)
            return None

        if maintenance and not (user.is_admin or user.id in settings.admin_ids):
            msg = f"🛠 {maint_text}"
            if isinstance(event, Message):
                await event.answer(msg)
            elif isinstance(event, CallbackQuery):
                await event.answer(maint_text, show_alert=True)
            return None

        data["user"] = user
        return await handler(event, data)

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.db import session_scope
from app.core.models import Chat, Message, User, utcnow


async def active_chat_id(user_id: int, *, model: str | None = None) -> int:
    """Последний чат бота; если его нет — создаётся новый."""
    async with session_scope() as s:
        row = await s.execute(
            select(Chat)
            .where(Chat.user_id == user_id, Chat.source == "bot", Chat.is_deleted == False)  # noqa: E712
            .order_by(Chat.updated_at.desc())
            .limit(1)
        )
        chat = row.scalar_one_or_none()
        if chat is not None:
            return chat.id
        user = await s.get(User, user_id)
        path = model or (user.current_model if user else None) or catalog.default_model(
            "pro" if user and user.is_pro else "free"
        ).path
        chat = Chat(user_id=user_id, title="Новый чат", model=path, source="bot")
        s.add(chat)
        await s.flush()
        return chat.id


async def new_chat(user_id: int) -> int:
    async with session_scope() as s:
        user = await s.get(User, user_id)
        path = (user.current_model if user else None) or catalog.default_model("free").path
        chat = Chat(user_id=user_id, title="Новый чат", model=path, source="bot")
        s.add(chat)
        await s.flush()
        return chat.id


async def chat_len(chat_id: int) -> int:
    async with session_scope() as s:
        return int(await s.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(Message).where(Message.chat_id == chat_id)
        ) or 0)


async def set_model(user_id: int, path: str) -> None:
    async with session_scope() as s:
        user = await s.get(User, user_id)
        if user is None:
            return
        user.current_model = path
        row = await s.execute(
            select(Chat)
            .where(Chat.user_id == user_id, Chat.source == "bot", Chat.is_deleted == False)  # noqa: E712
            .order_by(Chat.updated_at.desc()).limit(1)
        )
        chat = row.scalar_one_or_none()
        if chat is not None:
            chat.model = path
            chat.updated_at = utcnow()


async def fresh_user(user_id: int) -> User | None:
    async with session_scope() as s:
        return await s.get(User, user_id)

FILE: app/bot/streamer.py
================================================
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
import time

from aiogram import Bot
from aiogram.exceptions import TelegramBadRequest, TelegramRetryAfter
from aiogram.types import InlineKeyboardMarkup, Message

from app.services.tgformat import md_to_html, preview_for_stream, split_html

log = logging.getLogger("bot.stream")

MIN_INTERVAL = 1.2
CURSOR = " ▍"

PHASE_ICONS = {
    "connecting": "⏳",
    "thinking": "💭",
    "reasoning": "🧩",
    "searching": "🌐",
    "vision": "🖼",
    "typing": "✍️",
}


class Streamer:
    """Один ответ модели = одно сообщение, которое превращается из статуса в текст."""

    def __init__(self, bot: Bot, message: Message, markup: InlineKeyboardMarkup | None = None):
        self.bot = bot
        self.msg = message
        self.markup = markup
        self.text = ""
        self.label = "думает"
        self.icon = "💭"
        self._last_edit = 0.0
        self._rendered = ""
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    async def set_phase(self, phase: str, label: str) -> None:
        self.label = label
        self.icon = PHASE_ICONS.get(phase, "⏳")
        if not self.text:
            await self._render(force=True)

    async def add(self, chunk: str) -> None:
        self.text += chunk
        await self._render()

    def _body(self, final: bool = False) -> str:
        if not self.text:
            return f"<i>{self.icon} {self.label}…</i>"
        if final:
            return md_to_html(self.text)
        return preview_for_stream(self.text) + CURSOR

    async def _render(self, force: bool = False) -> None:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if not force and now - self._last_edit < MIN_INTERVAL:
            return
        async with self._lock:
            body = self._body()
            if body == self._rendered:
                return
            self._last_edit = time.monotonic()
            await self._edit(body, self.markup)
            self._rendered = body

    async def _edit(self, text: str, markup: InlineKeyboardMarkup | None) -> None:
        try:
            await self.msg.edit_text(text, reply_markup=markup, disable_web_page_preview=True)
        except TelegramRetryAfter as e:
            self._last_edit = time.monotonic() + e.retry_after
        except TelegramBadRequest as e:
            if "not modified" in str(e).lower():
                return
            log.warning("edit failed: %s", e)
            try:  # сломалась разметка — отдаём текстом, лишь бы ответ дошёл
                await self.msg.edit_text(self.text[:4000], reply_markup=markup)
            except TelegramBadRequest:
                pass

    async def finish(self, footer: str = "", citations: list[dict] | None = None) -> None:
        """Финальный рендер: длинный ответ уезжает дополнительными сообщениями."""
        async with self._lock:
            body = md_to_html(self.text) if self.text else "<i>Пустой ответ модели.</i>"
            # Источники теперь отображаются только в web интерфейсе
            chunks = split_html(body) or [body]

            await self._edit(chunks[0] + (f"\n\n{footer}" if footer and len(chunks) == 1 else ""), None)
            for i, part in enumerate(chunks[1:], start=1):
                tail = f"\n\n{footer}" if footer and i == len(chunks) - 1 else ""
                try:
                    await self.bot.send_message(
                        self.msg.chat.id, part + tail, disable_web_page_preview=True
                    )
                except TelegramBadRequest as e:
                    log.warning("send tail failed: %s", e)
                await asyncio.sleep(0.3)

    async def fail(self, text: str, markup: InlineKeyboardMarkup | None = None) -> None:
        async with self._lock:
            if self.text:
                await self._edit(md_to_html(self.text) + f"\n\n<i>{text}</i>", markup)
            else:
                await self._edit(f"⚠️ {text}", markup)


def _short(s: str, n: int = 60) -> str:
    s = " ".join(s.split())
    return s[:n] + ("…" if len(s) > n else "")

from aiogram import Router

from app.bot.handlers import billing, chat, common, models


def setup_routers() -> Router:
    root = Router(name="root")
    # порядок важен: промокод-состояние и команды идут раньше свободного текста
    root.include_router(common.router)
    root.include_router(billing.router)
    root.include_router(models.router)
    root.include_router(chat.router)
    return root

FILE: app/bot/handlers/chat.py
