from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any

from aiogram import Bot, F, Router
from aiogram.enums import ChatAction
from aiogram.filters import Command
from aiogram.types import CallbackQuery, Message

from app.bot import service
from app.bot.keyboards import pro_kb, stop_kb
from app.bot.streamer import Streamer
from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.db import session_scope
from app.core.models import User
from app.services import files as files_svc, pipeline
from app.services.limits import busy_users, limiter

log = logging.getLogger("bot.chat")
router = Router(name="chat")

MAX_INPUT_CHARS = 20_000
ALBUM_DELAY = 0.8

_tasks: dict[int, asyncio.Task] = {}
_albums: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}


# ── приём сообщений ────────────────────────────────────────
@router.message(Command("stop"))
async def stop_cmd(message: Message, user: User) -> None:
    task = _tasks.get(user.id)
    if task and not task.done():
        task.cancel()
        await message.answer("⏹ Остановил.")
    else:
        await message.answer("Сейчас ничего не генерируется.")


@router.callback_query(F.data == "stop")
async def stop_cb(call: CallbackQuery, user: User) -> None:
    task = _tasks.get(user.id)
    if task and not task.done():
        task.cancel()
        await call.answer("Остановлено")
    else:
        await call.answer("Уже готово")


@router.message(F.text & ~F.text.startswith("/"))
async def on_text(message: Message, user: User, bot: Bot) -> None:
    text = (message.text or "").strip()
    if len(text) > MAX_INPUT_CHARS:
        await message.answer(
            f"Сообщение длиннее {MAX_INPUT_CHARS} символов. "
            "Пришлите его файлом — я прочитаю документ."
        )
        return
    await generate(bot, message, user, text, [])


@router.message(F.photo | F.document | F.caption)
async def on_media(message: Message, user: User, bot: Bot) -> None:
    if message.media_group_id:
        await _buffer_album(bot, message, user)
        return
    att = await _save_attachment(bot, message, user)
    if att is None:
        return
    await generate(bot, message, user, (message.caption or "").strip(), [att])


async def _buffer_album(bot: Bot, message: Message, user: User) -> None:
    """Альбом приходит отдельными апдейтами: собираем по media_group_id."""
    key = f"{message.chat.id}:{message.media_group_id}"
    box = _albums.setdefault(key, {"messages": [], "task": None})
    box["messages"].append(message)
    if box["task"]:
        box["task"].cancel()

    async def flush() -> None:
        try:
            await asyncio.sleep(ALBUM_DELAY)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            return
        group = _albums.pop(key, {}).get("messages") or []
        if not group:
            return
        plan = catalog.plan("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")
        limit = max(plan.max_attachments, 1)
        atts: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for m in group[:limit]:
            att = await _save_attachment(bot, m, user, quiet=True)
            if att:
                atts.append(att)
        if not atts:
            await group[0].answer("Не удалось принять файлы. Вложения доступны в Pro.",
                                  reply_markup=pro_kb())
            return
        caption = next((m.caption for m in group if m.caption), "") or ""
        if len(group) > limit:
            await group[0].answer(f"Взял первые {limit} вложения из {len(group)}.")
        await generate(bot, group[-1], user, caption.strip(), atts)

    box["task"] = asyncio.create_task(flush())


async def _save_attachment(
    bot: Bot, message: Message, user: User, *, quiet: bool = False
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    plan = catalog.plan("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")
    if plan.max_attachments <= 0:
        if not quiet:
            await message.answer(
                "📎 Вложения доступны в <b>Pro</b>. Текстом отвечу на любом тарифе.",
                reply_markup=pro_kb(),
            )
        return None

    if message.photo:
        tg_file = message.photo[-1]
        name = f"photo_{message.message_id}.jpg"
        mime = "image/jpeg"
    elif message.document:
        tg_file = message.document
        name = message.document.file_name or f"file_{message.message_id}"
        mime = message.document.mime_type or "application/octet-stream"
    else:
        return None

    size = getattr(tg_file, "file_size", 0) or 0
    if size > 20 * 1024 * 1024:  # лимит Bot API на скачивание
        if not quiet:
            await message.answer("Файл больше 20 МБ — Telegram не даёт его скачать боту.")
        return None

    try:
        buf = await bot.download(tg_file)
        data = buf.read()
    except Exception as e:
        log.warning("download failed: %s", e)
        if not quiet:
            await message.answer("Не смог скачать файл из Telegram. Попробуйте ещё раз.")
        return None

    try:
        async with session_scope() as s:
            att = await files_svc.save_upload(
                s, user_id=user.id, data=data, file_name=name, mime=mime
            )
            payload = {"id": att.id, "kind": att.kind, "name": att.file_name, "mime": att.mime}
    except ValueError as e:
        if not quiet:
            await message.answer(f"⚠️ {e}")
        return None

    if payload["kind"] == "doc" and not att.text_excerpt:
        if not quiet:
            await message.answer(
                "Из этого файла не получилось извлечь текст. "
                "Понимаю PDF, DOCX, TXT, CSV, JSON и код."
            )
    return payload


# ── генерация ──────────────────────────────────────────────
async def generate(
    bot: Bot, message: Message, user: User, text: str, atts: list[dict[str, Any]]
) -> None:
    if not text and not atts:
        return

    if user.id in busy_users or (user.id in _tasks and not _tasks[user.id].done()):
        await message.answer("Дождитесь предыдущего ответа или нажмите /stop.")
        return
    if not limiter.check(user.id):
        await message.answer(f"Слишком часто. Подождите {limiter.retry_after(user.id)} с.")
        return

    plan = catalog.plan("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")
    model = catalog.get(user.current_model) or catalog.default_model(plan.code)

    if not energy_svc.can_afford(user, model):
        await message.answer(
            "⚡️ Закончилась энергия.\n"
            f"Бесплатные модели остаются доступными: "
            f"{', '.join(m.name for m in catalog.models_for(plan.code) if m.free) or '—'}.",
            reply_markup=pro_kb(),
        )
        return

    has_image = any(a["kind"] == "image" for a in atts)
    if has_image and not model.vision:
        vision = [m.name for m in catalog.models_for(plan.code) if m.vision]
        await message.answer(
            f"<b>{model.name}</b> не смотрит картинки — опишу по тексту.\n"
            + (f"Модели с 👁: {', '.join(vision)} (/model)" if vision else
               "Модели с поддержкой картинок есть в Pro.")
        )

    chat_id = await service.active_chat_id(user.id, model=model.path)
    placeholder = await message.answer("<i>⏳ подключаюсь…</i>", reply_markup=stop_kb())
    streamer = Streamer(bot, placeholder, stop_kb())

    task = asyncio.create_task(
        _run(bot, message, user, streamer, chat_id, text, atts, model.path)
    )
    _tasks[user.id] = task
    try:
        await task
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        pass
    finally:
        _tasks.pop(user.id, None)


async def _run(
    bot: Bot,
    message: Message,
    user: User,
    streamer: Streamer,
    chat_id: int,
    text: str,
    atts: list[dict[str, Any]],
    model_path: str,
) -> None:
    gen = pipeline.run(
        user_id=user.id,
        chat_id=chat_id,
        text=text,
        attachments=atts,
        source="bot",
        model_path=model_path,
    )
    citations: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    busy_users.add(user.id)
    typing = asyncio.create_task(_keep_typing(bot, message.chat.id))

    try:
        async for ev in gen:
            kind = ev.get("type")
            if kind == "phase":
                await streamer.set_phase(ev["phase"], ev["label"])
            elif kind == "delta":
                await streamer.add(ev["text"])
            elif kind == "citations":
                citations.extend(ev["items"])
            elif kind == "error":
                kb = pro_kb() if ev.get("code") in ("need_pro", "no_energy") else None
                await streamer.fail(ev["message"], kb)
                return
            elif kind == "done":
                await streamer.finish(
                    footer=_footer(ev, model_path, truncated=ev.get("truncated")),
                    citations=citations or ev.get("citations") or [],
                )
                return
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        await gen.aclose()
        await streamer.finish(footer="<i>⏹ остановлено</i>", citations=citations)
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        log.exception("bot generation crashed uid=%s", user.id)
        await streamer.fail(f"Внутренняя ошибка: {e}")
    finally:
        typing.cancel()
        busy_users.discard(user.id)


async def _keep_typing(bot: Bot, chat_id: int) -> None:
    try:
        while True:
            await bot.send_chat_action(chat_id, ChatAction.TYPING)
            await asyncio.sleep(4.5)
    except (asyncio.CancelledError, Exception):
        return


def _footer(ev: dict[str, Any], model_path: str, truncated: bool = False) -> str:
    model = catalog.get(model_path)
    cost = energy_svc.to_energy(int(ev.get("cost") or 0))
    parts = [f"<i>{model.name if model else model_path}"]
    if cost:
        parts.append(f"−{cost:g} энергии")
    parts.append(f"осталось {ev.get('energy', 0)}")
    # searches больше не показываем в footer - источники отображаются отдельно
    if truncated:
        parts.append("ответ прерван провайдером")
    return " · ".join(parts) + "</i>"

from __future__ import annotations

from aiogram import F, Router
from aiogram.types import CallbackQuery, Message

from app.bot import service
from app.bot.handlers.billing import send_paywall
from app.bot.keyboards import models_kb
from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.models import User
from aiogram.filters import Command

router = Router(name="models")


def _header(user: User) -> str:
    plan = catalog.plan("pro" if user.is_pro else "free")
    return (
        f"<b>Выберите модель</b>\n"
        f"Баланс: {energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens)} из {plan.energy} энергии\n"
        f"<i>×коэффициент — во столько раз запрос дороже базового</i>"
    )


@router.message(Command("model"))
@router.message(F.text == "🧠 Модель")
async def choose(message: Message, user: User) -> None:
    plan = "pro" if user.is_pro else "free"
    await message.answer(_header(user), reply_markup=models_kb(plan, user.current_model))


@router.callback_query(F.data.startswith("m:"))
async def pick(call: CallbackQuery, user: User) -> None:
    idx = int(call.data.split(":")[1])
    models = catalog.models()
    if idx >= len(models):
        await call.answer("Модель больше недоступна", show_alert=True)
        return
    model = models[idx]
    plan = "pro" if user.is_pro else "free"

    if not model.allowed_for(plan):
        await call.answer("Эта модель доступна в Pro", show_alert=True)
        await send_paywall(call.message, user)
        return

    await service.set_model(user.id, model.path)
    await call.answer(f"Модель: {model.name}")
    try:
        await call.message.edit_text(_header(user), reply_markup=models_kb(plan, model.path))
    except Exception:
        pass

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml

from app.core.config import settings


@dataclass(slots=True)
class ModelSpec:
    path: str
    name: str
    short: str
    multiplier: float
    plans: tuple[str, ...]
    vision: bool = False
    reasoning: bool = False
    web_search: bool = False
    context: int = 128_000
    default: bool = False

    @property
    def free(self) -> bool:
        return self.multiplier == 0

    def allowed_for(self, plan: str) -> bool:
        return plan in self.plans

    def to_public(self, plan: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "path": self.path,
            "name": self.name,
            "short": self.short,
            "multiplier": self.multiplier,
            "vision": self.vision,
            "reasoning": self.reasoning,
            "web_search": self.web_search,
            "context": self.context,
            "locked": not self.allowed_for(plan),
            "pro": "free" not in self.plans,
        }


@dataclass(slots=True)
class PlanSpec:
    code: str
    title: str
    energy: int
    price_rub: int = 0
    days: int = 0
    refill_days: int = 0
    max_attachments: int = 0
    context_messages: int = 10
    web_search: bool = False
    features: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)


class _Catalog:
    """Ленивая загрузка конфигов с проверкой mtime."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._mtimes: dict[Path, float] = {}
        self._models: list[ModelSpec] = []
        self._plans: dict[str, PlanSpec] = {}
        self._system_prompt = ""
        self._energy_unit = settings.tokens_per_energy

    # ── файлы ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    @property
    def models_file(self) -> Path:
        return settings.config_dir / "models.yml"

    @property
    def plans_file(self) -> Path:
        return settings.config_dir / "plans.yml"

    def _stale(self) -> bool:
        for p in (self.models_file, self.plans_file):
            try:
                m = p.stat().st_mtime
            except FileNotFoundError:
                continue
            if self._mtimes.get(p) != m:
                return True
        return False

    def reload(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            raw_models = _read_yaml(self.models_file)
            raw_plans = _read_yaml(self.plans_file)

            models: list[ModelSpec] = []
            for item in raw_models.get("models", []):
                models.append(
                    ModelSpec(
                        path=str(item["path"]),
                        name=str(item.get("name") or item["path"]),
                        short=str(item.get("short") or item.get("name") or item["path"]),
                        multiplier=float(_num(item.get("multiplier"), 1)),
                        plans=tuple(str(x) for x in (item.get("plans") or ["pro"])),
                        vision=bool(item.get("vision", False)),
                        reasoning=bool(item.get("reasoning", False)),
                        web_search=bool(item.get("web_search", False)),
                        context=int(_num(item.get("context"), 128_000)),
                        default=bool(item.get("default", False)),
                    )
                )

            plans: dict[str, PlanSpec] = {}
            for code, item in (raw_plans.get("plans") or {}).items():
                plans[code] = PlanSpec(
                    code=code,
                    title=str(item.get("title", code.title())),
                    energy=int(_num(item.get("energy"), 0)),
                    price_rub=int(_num(item.get("price_rub"), 0)),
                    days=int(_num(item.get("days"), 0)),
                    refill_days=int(_num(item.get("refill_days"), 0)),
                    max_attachments=int(_num(item.get("max_attachments"), 0)),
                    context_messages=int(_num(item.get("context_messages"), 10)),
                    web_search=bool(item.get("web_search", False)),
                    features=tuple(item.get("features") or ()),
                )

            self._models = models
            self._plans = plans
            self._system_prompt = str(raw_models.get("system_prompt", "")).strip()
            self._energy_unit = int(_num(raw_plans.get("energy_unit"), settings.tokens_per_energy))
            for p in (self.models_file, self.plans_file):
                if p.exists():
                    self._mtimes[p] = p.stat().st_mtime

    def _ensure(self) -> None:
        if not self._models or self._stale():
            self.reload()

    # ── доступ ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    @property
    def energy_unit(self) -> int:
        self._ensure()
        return self._energy_unit or settings.tokens_per_energy

    @property
    def system_prompt(self) -> str:
        self._ensure()
        return self._system_prompt

    def models(self) -> list[ModelSpec]:
        self._ensure()
        return list(self._models)

    def models_for(self, plan: str) -> list[ModelSpec]:
        return [m for m in self.models() if m.allowed_for(plan)]

    def get(self, path: str | None) -> ModelSpec | None:
        if not path:
            return None
        self._ensure()
        return next((m for m in self._models if m.path == path), None)

    def default_model(self, plan: str = "free") -> ModelSpec:
        allowed = self.models_for(plan) or self.models()
        return next((m for m in allowed if m.default), allowed[0])

    def plans(self) -> dict[str, PlanSpec]:
        self._ensure()
        return dict(self._plans)

    def plan(self, code: str) -> PlanSpec:
        self._ensure()
        return self._plans.get(code) or self._plans.get("free") or PlanSpec(
            code="free", title="Free", energy=50
        )

    def pro(self) -> PlanSpec:
        return self.plan("pro")

    # ── правка из админки ───────────────────────────────────
    def raw_text(self, which: str) -> str:
        path = self.models_file if which == "models" else self.plans_file
        return path.read_text("utf-8") if path.exists() else ""

    def save_text(self, which: str, text: str) -> None:
        """Пишет YAML только если он парсится и проходит полную проверку."""
        data = yaml.safe_load(text) or {}
        if which == "models":
            items = data.get("models")
            if not isinstance(items, list) or not items:
                raise ValueError("в models.yml нужен непустой список 'models'")
            seen: set[str] = set()
            for i, m in enumerate(items, 1):
                if not isinstance(m, dict):
                    raise ValueError(f"модель #{i}: должна быть набором полей key: value")
                for key in ("path", "name", "multiplier", "plans"):
                    if key not in m:
                        raise ValueError(f"модель #{i}: нет обязательного поля '{key}'")
                path = str(m["path"]).strip()
                if not path:
                    raise ValueError(f"модель #{i}: пустой path")
                if path in seen:
                    raise ValueError(f"модель #{i}: путь '{path}' повторяется")
                seen.add(path)
                try:
                    mult = float(_num(m["multiplier"], 1))
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    raise ValueError(f"{path}: multiplier должен быть числом, а не '{m['multiplier']}'")
                if mult < 0:
                    raise ValueError(f"{path}: multiplier не может быть отрицательным")
                plans = m["plans"]
                if not isinstance(plans, list) or not plans:
                    raise ValueError(f"{path}: plans должен быть списком, например [free, pro]")
                known = set((yaml.safe_load(self.raw_text("plans")) or {}).get("plans") or {})
                bad = [str(x) for x in plans if str(x) not in known]
                if bad:
                    raise ValueError(f"{path}: неизвестные тарифы {bad}, есть {sorted(known)}")
            if not any(m.get("plans") and "free" in m["plans"] and float(m.get("multiplier", 1)) == 0
                       for m in items):
                raise ValueError("нужна хотя бы одна бесплатная модель с тарифом free")
        elif which == "plans":
            plans = data.get("plans")
            if not isinstance(plans, dict) or "free" not in plans:
                raise ValueError("в plans.yml нужен ключ 'plans' с тарифом 'free'")
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"неизвестный файл '{which}'")

        path_file = self.models_file if which == "models" else self.plans_file
        backup = path_file.with_suffix(path_file.suffix + ".bak")
        if path_file.exists():
            backup.write_text(path_file.read_text("utf-8"), "utf-8")
        path_file.write_text(text, "utf-8")
        try:
            self.reload()
        except Exception as e:
            # откат: иначе приложение не поднимется после перезапуска
            if backup.exists():
                path_file.write_text(backup.read_text("utf-8"), "utf-8")
                self.reload()
            raise ValueError(f"конфиг отклонён и возвращён прежний: {e}") from e


def _num(value, default):
    """YAML null и пустая строка означают «не задано»."""
    if value is None or value == "":
        return default
    return value


def _read_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    return yaml.safe_load(path.read_text("utf-8")) or {}


catalog = _Catalog()

                if not path:
                    raise ValueError(f"модель #{i}: пустой path")

                    raise ValueError(f"модель #{i}: путь '{path}' повторяется")
                seen.add(path)
                try:

                known = set((yaml.safe_load(self.raw_text("plans")) or {}).get("plans") or {})

                       for m in items):

        elif which == "plans":
            plans = data.get("plans")
            if not isinstance(plans, dict) or "free" not in plans:
                raise ValueError("в plans.yml нужен ключ 'plans' с тарифом 'free'")
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"неизвестный файл '{which}'")

        path_file.write_text(text, "utf-8")
        try:
            self.reload()
        except Exception as e:
            # откат: иначе приложение не поднимется после перезапуска
            if backup.exists():
                path_file.write_text(backup.read_text("utf-8"), "utf-8")
                self.reload()
            raise ValueError(f"конфиг отклонён и возвращён прежний: {e}") from e


def _read_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    return yaml.safe_load(path.read_text("utf-8")) or {}


catalog = _Catalog()

from __future__ import annotations

from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import AliasChoices, Field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=ROOT / ".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        extra="ignore",
        case_sensitive=False,
    )

    # ── Telegram ───────────────────────────────────────────
    bot_token: str = ""
    bot_username: str = ""
    webapp_url: str = "http://localhost:8080"
    # Читается строкой: иначе pydantic-settings пытается разобрать значение
    # как JSON и одиночный id превращается в int, а не в список.
    admin_ids_raw: str = Field(
        default="",
        validation_alias=AliasChoices("ADMIN_IDS", "admin_ids", "admin_ids_raw"),
    )

    # ── AnyModel ───────────────────────────────────────────
    anymodel_api_key: str = ""
    anymodel_base_url: str = "https://anymodel.org/v1"

    # ── Platega ────────────────────────────────────────────
    platega_merchant_id: str = ""
    platega_secret: str = ""
    platega_base_url: str = "https://app.platega.io"
    platega_payment_method: int = 2
    platega_callback_secret_check: bool = True

    # ── Ссылки для Mini App и бота (можно переопределить в админке) ──────
    support_url: str = ""          # https://t.me/your_support
    share_text: str = ""           # текст при «поделиться ботом»

    # ── Приложение ─────────────────────────────────────────
    secret_key: str = "dev-secret-change-me"
    database_url: str = "sqlite+aiosqlite:///./data/blrd.db"
    data_dir: Path = ROOT / "data"
    public_host: str = "0.0.0.0"
    port: int = 8080
    log_level: str = "INFO"
    # Локальная отладка: вход в веб без Telegram под этим id.
    # В продакшене оставить 0 — иначе любой получит доступ к аккаунту.
    dev_auth_id: int = 1

    # ── Админка ────────────────────────────────────────────
    admin_password: str = "admin"

    # ── Экономика ──────────────────────────────────────────
    tokens_per_energy: int = 3000
    web_search_base_cost: int = 40_000
    max_upload_mb: int = 20

    # ── производные значения ───────────────────────────────
    @property
    def admin_ids(self) -> list[int]:
        """ADMIN_IDS=111,222 или 111;222 или одиночный id — всё равно список."""
        raw = str(self.admin_ids_raw or "").strip().strip("[]")
        out: list[int] = []
        for part in raw.replace(";", ",").replace(" ", ",").split(","):
            part = part.strip().strip("'\"")
            if not part:
                continue
            try:
                out.append(int(part))
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return out

    @property
    def uploads_dir(self) -> Path:
        p = self.data_dir / "uploads"
        p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return p

    @property
    def config_dir(self) -> Path:
        return ROOT / "config"

    @property
    def web_dir(self) -> Path:
        return ROOT / "web"

    @property
    def callback_url(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.webapp_url.rstrip('/')}/api/payments/platega/callback"

    @property
    def sqlite(self) -> bool:
        return self.database_url.startswith("sqlite")


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    s = Settings()
    s.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return s


settings = get_settings()

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker, create_async_engine

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.models import Base

_url = settings.database_url
_kw: dict = {"echo": False, "pool_pre_ping": True}
if _url.startswith("sqlite"):
    _kw.pop("pool_pre_ping")
    _kw["connect_args"] = {"timeout": 30}

engine = create_async_engine(_url, **_kw)
SessionLocal = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False, class_=AsyncSession)


async def init_db() -> None:
    from sqlalchemy import text

    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        if _url.startswith("sqlite"):
            await conn.execute(text("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL"))
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)


@asynccontextmanager
async def session_scope() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
    """Для бота и фоновых задач."""
    async with SessionLocal() as s:
        try:
            yield s
            await s.commit()
        except Exception:
            await s.rollback()
            raise


async def get_session() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:
    """FastAPI dependency."""
    async with SessionLocal() as s:
        try:
            yield s
            await s.commit()
        except Exception:
            await s.rollback()
            raise

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import math

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.catalog import ModelSpec, catalog
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.models import UsageLog, User, utcnow


def unit() -> int:
    return catalog.energy_unit or settings.tokens_per_energy


def to_energy(base_tokens: int) -> float:
    return round(base_tokens / unit(), 2)


def to_base(energy: float) -> int:
    return int(round(energy * unit()))


def energy_display(base_tokens: int) -> int:
    """Целое число энергии для интерфейса (вниз, чтобы не обещать лишнего)."""
    return max(0, int(base_tokens // unit()))


def price_base(model: ModelSpec, tokens_in: int, tokens_out: int, searches: int = 0) -> int:
    """Базовые токены за запрос: единый множитель на вход и выход."""
    if model.multiplier <= 0:
        return searches * settings.web_search_base_cost
    cost = math.ceil((tokens_in + tokens_out) * model.multiplier)
    cost += searches * settings.web_search_base_cost
    return max(1, cost)


def plan_quota_base(plan_code: str) -> int:
    return to_base(catalog.plan(plan_code).energy)


def can_afford(user: User, model: ModelSpec) -> bool:
    """Бесплатные модели не требуют баланса; платным нужна хотя бы 1 энергия."""
    if model.multiplier <= 0:
        return True
    return user.energy_tokens >= unit()


async def charge(
    session: AsyncSession,
    user: User,
    model: ModelSpec,
    tokens_in: int,
    tokens_out: int,
    *,
    searches: int = 0,
    source: str = "web",
    ok: bool = True,
) -> int:
    """Списывает базовые токены и пишет лог. Возвращает списанное."""
    cost = price_base(model, tokens_in, tokens_out, searches)
    user.energy_tokens = max(0, user.energy_tokens - cost)
    user.spent_tokens = (user.spent_tokens or 0) + cost
    user.requests = (user.requests or 0) + 1
    user.last_seen = utcnow()
    session.add(
        UsageLog(
            user_id=user.id, model=model.path, tokens_in=tokens_in,
            tokens_out=tokens_out, base_cost=cost, searches=searches,
            source=source, ok=ok,
        )
    )
    await session.flush()
    return cost


def ensure_plan_state(user: User) -> bool:
    """
    Приводит тариф и баланс в актуальное состояние:
    истёк Pro -> Free, подошёл срок автопополнения Free -> пополнить.
    Возвращает True, если что-то изменилось.
    """
    changed = False
    now = utcnow()

    if user.plan == "pro" and user.plan_until and user.plan_until <= now:
        user.plan = "free"
        user.plan_until = None
        user.energy_tokens = plan_quota_base("free")
        user.refill_at = now + dt.timedelta(days=catalog.plan("free").refill_days or 30)
        changed = True

    if user.plan == "free":
        if user.refill_at is None:
            days = catalog.plan("free").refill_days or 30
            user.refill_at = now + dt.timedelta(days=days)
            changed = True
        elif user.refill_at <= now:
            user.energy_tokens = max(user.energy_tokens, plan_quota_base("free"))
            days = catalog.plan("free").refill_days or 30
            user.refill_at = now + dt.timedelta(days=days)
            changed = True

    # модель из конфига могла стать недоступной тарифу
    m = catalog.get(user.current_model)
    if m is None or not m.allowed_for(user.plan):
        user.current_model = catalog.default_model(user.plan).path
        changed = True

    return changed


def grant_pro(user: User, days: int | None = None, *, add_energy: bool = True) -> None:
    """Продлевает Pro (накопительно) и выдаёт месячную квоту энергии."""
    pro = catalog.pro()
    days = days or pro.days or 30
    now = utcnow()
    base = user.plan_until if (user.plan == "pro" and user.plan_until and user.plan_until > now) else now
    user.plan = "pro"
    user.plan_until = base + dt.timedelta(days=days)
    user.refill_at = None
    if add_energy:
        user.energy_tokens = max(user.energy_tokens, 0) + plan_quota_base("pro")


def add_energy(user: User, energy: float) -> None:
    user.energy_tokens = max(0, user.energy_tokens + to_base(energy))

FILE: app/core/lock.py

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

from app.core.config import settings


class AlreadyRunning(RuntimeError):
    pass


def acquire(name: str = "bot") -> Path:
    """
    Один процесс на имя. Если PID из лока ещё жив — отказ;
    если умер (упал, убит), лок переписывается.
    """
    path = settings.data_dir / f"{name}.pid"
    if path.exists():
        try:
            old = int(path.read_text().strip() or 0)
        except ValueError:
            old = 0
        if old and old != os.getpid() and _alive(old):
            raise AlreadyRunning(
                f"процесс {name} уже запущен (PID {old}). "
                f"Остановите его или удалите {path}"
            )
    path.write_text(str(os.getpid()))
    return path


def release(path: Path) -> None:
    try:
        if path.exists() and path.read_text().strip() == str(os.getpid()):
            path.unlink()
    except OSError:
        pass


def _alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    if os.name == "nt":
        import ctypes

        PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION = 0x1000
        STILL_ACTIVE = 259
        handle = ctypes.windll.kernel32.OpenProcess(
            PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, pid
        )
        if not handle:
            return False
        try:
            code = ctypes.c_ulong()
            ok = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess(handle, ctypes.byref(code))
            return bool(ok) and code.value == STILL_ACTIVE
        finally:
            ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
        return True
    except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
        return False

﻿from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Index, Integer,
    JSON, String, Text, TypeDecorator, UniqueConstraint,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column, relationship


def utcnow() -> dt.datetime:
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc)


class UTCDateTime(TypeDecorator):
    """
    Р”Р°С‚Р° РІСЃРµРіРґР° РїСЂРёС…РѕРґРёС‚ РёР· Р‘Р” СЃ tzinfo=UTC.
    SQLite РЅРµ С…СЂР°РЅРёС‚ РїРѕСЏСЃ, Рё Р±РµР· СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ СЃСЂР°РІРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЃ utcnow() РїР°РґР°РµС‚
    РЅР° offset-naive vs offset-aware.
    """

    impl = DateTime(timezone=True)
    cache_ok = True

    def process_bind_param(self, value: dt.datetime | None, dialect) -> dt.datetime | None:
        if value is None:
            return None
        if value.tzinfo is None:
            return value.replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
        return value.astimezone(dt.timezone.utc)

    def process_result_value(self, value: dt.datetime | None, dialect) -> dt.datetime | None:
        if value is None:
            return None
        if value.tzinfo is None:
            return value.replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
        return value.astimezone(dt.timezone.utc)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    type_annotation_map = {dict[str, Any]: JSON, list[Any]: JSON}


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)  # telegram id
    username: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))
    first_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128))
    last_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128))
    photo_url: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(512))
    language: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(8), default="ru")

    plan: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="free")
    plan_until: Mapped[dt.datetime | None] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime())
    energy_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, default=0)   # Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Рµ С‚РѕРєРµРЅС‹
    refill_at: Mapped[dt.datetime | None] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime())

    current_model: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128))

    is_banned: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    ban_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(512))
    is_admin: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)

    requests: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    spent_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, default=0)
    paid_total: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)  # СЂСѓР±Р»Рё

    source: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(32))  # bot / web / ref
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)
    last_seen: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)

    chats: Mapped[list["Chat"]] = relationship(back_populates="user")

    @property
    def display_name(self) -> str:
        name = " ".join(x for x in (self.first_name, self.last_name) if x).strip()
        return name or (f"@{self.username}" if self.username else f"id{self.id}")

    @property
    def is_pro(self) -> bool:
        if self.plan != "pro":
            return False
        if self.plan_until is None:
            return True
        return self.plan_until > utcnow()


class Chat(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chats"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), default="РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‡Р°С‚")
    model: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128))
    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="web")  # web | bot
    is_deleted: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)

    user: Mapped[User] = relationship(back_populates="chats")
    messages: Mapped[list["Message"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="chat", cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        order_by="Message.id",
    )


class Message(Base):
    __tablename__ = "messages"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    chat_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, ForeignKey("chats.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True)
    role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))  # user | assistant | system
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    reasoning: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    model: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128))
    attachments: Mapped[list[Any] | None] = mapped_column(JSON)
    tokens_in: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    tokens_out: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    base_cost: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)

    chat: Mapped[Chat] = relationship(back_populates="messages")


class Attachment(Base):
    __tablename__ = "attachments"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), primary_key=True)  # uuid4 hex

    rel_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    text_excerpt: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)  # РґР»СЏ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)


class Payment(Base):

    tx_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), unique=True)  # id РІ Platega

    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="web")
    pay_url: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(600))
    raw: Mapped[dict[str, Any] | None] = mapped_column(JSON)
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)
    paid_at: Mapped[dt.datetime | None] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime())


class Promo(Base):
    __tablename__ = "promos"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)

    sent: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    failed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    total: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)
    finished_at: Mapped[dt.datetime | None] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime())


class Setting(Base):
    """Runtime-РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕР№РєРё: С‚РµС…СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‹, Р»РёРјРёС‚С‹, С‚РµРєСЃС‚С‹."""

    __tablename__ = "settings"

    key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    value: Mapped[dict[str, Any] | None] = mapped_column(JSON)
    updated_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(UTCDateTime(), default=utcnow)

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import time
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import parse_qsl

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request, status
from itsdangerous import BadSignature, URLSafeTimedSerializer
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.db import get_session
from app.core.energy import ensure_plan_state, plan_quota_base
from app.core.models import User, utcnow

INIT_DATA_TTL = 24 * 3600
SESSION_TTL = 30 * 24 * 3600

_session_signer = URLSafeTimedSerializer(settings.secret_key, salt="webapp-session")
_admin_signer = URLSafeTimedSerializer(settings.secret_key, salt="admin-session")


# ── Telegram initData ──────────────────────────────────────
def verify_init_data(init_data: str, *, ttl: int = INIT_DATA_TTL) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Проверяет подпись Mini App по алгоритму Telegram:
    secret = HMAC_SHA256("WebAppData", bot_token); hash = HMAC_SHA256(secret, data_check_string).
    """
    if not init_data:
        raise ValueError("empty init_data")
    pairs = dict(parse_qsl(init_data, keep_blank_values=True))
    received = pairs.pop("hash", None)
    if not received:
        raise ValueError("no hash")

    check_string = "\n".join(f"{k}={pairs[k]}" for k in sorted(pairs))
    secret = hmac.new(b"WebAppData", settings.bot_token.encode(), hashlib.sha256).digest()
    expected = hmac.new(secret, check_string.encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
    if not hmac.compare_digest(expected, received):
        raise ValueError("bad signature")

    auth_date = int(pairs.get("auth_date", "0") or 0)
    if ttl and auth_date and time.time() - auth_date > ttl:
        raise ValueError("init_data expired")

    user_raw = pairs.get("user")
    if not user_raw:
        raise ValueError("no user in init_data")
    pairs["user"] = json.loads(user_raw)
    return pairs


# ── сессии веб-приложения ──────────────────────────────────
def issue_session(user_id: int) -> str:
    return _session_signer.dumps({"uid": user_id})


def read_session(token: str) -> int | None:
    try:
        data = _session_signer.loads(token, max_age=SESSION_TTL)
        return int(data["uid"])
    except (BadSignature, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return None


# ── пользователи ───────────────────────────────────────────
async def upsert_user(
    session: AsyncSession,
    tg: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    source: str = "web",
) -> User:
    uid = int(tg["id"])
    user = await session.get(User, uid)
    created = user is None
    if created:
        user = User(
            id=uid,
            plan="free",
            energy_tokens=plan_quota_base("free"),
            source=source,
            is_admin=uid in settings.admin_ids,
        )
        session.add(user)

    user.username = tg.get("username") or user.username
    user.first_name = tg.get("first_name") or user.first_name
    user.last_name = tg.get("last_name") or user.last_name
    user.language = tg.get("language_code") or user.language
    if tg.get("photo_url"):
        user.photo_url = tg["photo_url"]
    if uid in settings.admin_ids:
        user.is_admin = True
    user.last_seen = utcnow()

    ensure_plan_state(user)
    await session.flush()
    return user


async def current_user(
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
) -> User:
    """
    Авторизация запроса веб-приложения. Принимает:
      - заголовок Authorization: tma <initData>  (первый запрос / мобильный клиент)
      - заголовок X-Session или cookie bs        (далее)
    """
    uid: int | None = None

    auth = request.headers.get("authorization") or ""
    if auth.lower().startswith("tma "):
        try:
            data = verify_init_data(auth[4:].strip())
        except ValueError as e:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, f"initData: {e}") from e
        user = await upsert_user(session, data["user"])
        _guard(user)
        return user

    token = request.headers.get("x-session") or request.cookies.get("bs")
    if token:
        uid = read_session(token)

    if uid is None:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, "not authenticated")

    user = await session.get(User, uid)
    if user is None:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, "user gone")
    if ensure_plan_state(user):
        await session.flush()
    _guard(user)
    return user


def _guard(user: User) -> None:
    if user.is_banned:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, user.ban_reason or "Доступ заблокирован")


# ── админка ────────────────────────────────────────────────
def issue_admin_session(login: str) -> str:
    return _admin_signer.dumps({"login": login})


def check_admin_cookie(token: str | None) -> bool:
    if not token:
        return False
    try:
        _admin_signer.loads(token, max_age=12 * 3600)
        return True
    except BadSignature:
        return False


async def require_admin(request: Request) -> bool:
    if not check_admin_cookie(request.cookies.get("badm")):
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_307_TEMPORARY_REDIRECT, headers={"Location": "/admin/login"})
    return True

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.models import Setting, utcnow

DEFAULTS: dict[str, Any] = {
    "maintenance": {"enabled": False, "message": "Идут технические работы, скоро вернёмся."},
    "signup_enabled": {"enabled": True},
    "welcome": {
        "text": "Привет! Я БЕЛЕБАЛАРДЫХ AI — доступ к лучшим моделям прямо в Telegram.\n"
                "Открой приложение кнопкой ниже или просто напиши мне сообщение."
    },
    "rate_limit": {"per_minute": 20},
    # пустые значения означают «взять из .env»
    "links": {"support_url": "", "share_text": "", "bot_username": ""},
}


async def get(session: AsyncSession, key: str) -> Any:
    row = await session.get(Setting, key)
    if row is None or row.value is None:
        return DEFAULTS.get(key)
    merged = dict(DEFAULTS.get(key) or {})
    merged.update(row.value)
    return merged


async def put(session: AsyncSession, key: str, value: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    row = await session.get(Setting, key)
    if row is None:
        session.add(Setting(key=key, value=value))
    else:
        row.value = value
        row.updated_at = utcnow()
    await session.flush()


async def maintenance(session: AsyncSession) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    data = await get(session, "maintenance") or {}
    return bool(data.get("enabled")), str(data.get("message") or "")


async def links(session: AsyncSession) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Ссылки с подстановкой значений из .env, если в админке не заданы."""
    from app.core.config import settings

    data = await get(session, "links") or {}
    return {
        "bot_username": (data.get("bot_username") or settings.bot_username or "").lstrip("@"),
        "support_url": data.get("support_url") or settings.support_url or "",
        "share_text": data.get("share_text") or settings.share_text
                      or "Все топовые нейросети в одном боте",
    }

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, AsyncIterator

import httpx
import orjson

from app.core.config import settings

log = logging.getLogger("anymodel")

# Фазы, которые уходят в интерфейс как «пишет / рассуждает / ищет…»
PHASE_CONNECT = "connecting"
PHASE_THINKING = "thinking"
PHASE_REASONING = "reasoning"
PHASE_SEARCHING = "searching"
PHASE_VISION = "vision"
PHASE_TYPING = "typing"
PHASE_DONE = "done"


class AnyModelError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, status: int = 0, code: str = "", request_id: str = ""):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status
        self.code = code
        self.request_id = request_id

    @property
    def user_text(self) -> str:
        if self.status == 402:
            return "У провайдера закончился баланс. Напишите в поддержку."
        if self.status == 429:
            return "Слишком много запросов к модели. Попробуйте через минуту."
        if self.status in (404, 406):
            return "Эта модель сейчас недоступна. Выберите другую."
        if self.status == 400 and self.code == "output_budget_exhausted":
            return "Ответ не поместился в лимит. Начните новый чат или сократите вопрос."
        if self.status >= 500 or self.status == 0:
            return "Провайдер не ответил. Попробуйте ещё раз."
        return f"Ошибка модели: {self}"


@dataclass(slots=True)
class StreamEvent:
    """Одно событие стрима: либо смена фазы, либо текст, либо финал."""

    type: str  # phase | delta | reasoning | citations | usage
    phase: str = ""
    text: str = ""
    citations: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    usage: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(slots=True)
class Usage:
    prompt_tokens: int = 0
    completion_tokens: int = 0
    searches: int = 0
    estimated: bool = False


class AnyModelClient:
    """
    Тонкая обёртка над OpenAI-совместимым /v1/chat/completions AnyModel.
    Один httpx-клиент на процесс: keep-alive важен для стрима.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    async def client(self) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
        if self._client is None or self._client.is_closed:
            async with self._lock:
                if self._client is None or self._client.is_closed:
                    self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
                        base_url=settings.anymodel_base_url.rstrip("/"),
                        headers={
                            "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.anymodel_api_key}",
                            "Content-Type": "application/json",
                        },
                        timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=20.0, read=600.0, write=60.0, pool=20.0),
                        limits=httpx.Limits(max_connections=100, max_keepalive_connections=40),
                    )
        return self._client

    async def close(self) -> None:
        if self._client and not self._client.is_closed:
            await self._client.aclose()

    # ── вспомогательное ─────────────────────────────────────
    async def balance(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        c = await self.client()
        r = await c.get("/balance")
        _raise_for_error(r)
        return r.json()

    async def catalog(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        c = await self.client()
        r = await c.get("/models")
        _raise_for_error(r)
        return r.json().get("data", [])

    # ── основной стрим ──────────────────────────────────────
    async def stream_chat(
        self,
        *,
        model: str,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        temperature: float | None = None,
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
        reasoning_effort: str | None = None,
        web_search: bool = False,
        has_images: bool = False,
    ) -> AsyncIterator[StreamEvent]:
        body: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": messages,
            "stream": True,
            "stream_options": {"include_usage": True},
        }
        if temperature is not None:
            body["temperature"] = temperature
        if max_tokens:
            body["max_tokens"] = max_tokens
        if reasoning_effort:
            body["reasoning_effort"] = reasoning_effort
        if web_search:
            body["tools"] = [{"type": "web_search"}]

        c = await self.client()
        phase = PHASE_VISION if has_images else PHASE_CONNECT
        yield StreamEvent("phase", phase=phase)

        text_started = False
        reasoning_started = False
        searching = False
        usage: dict[str, Any] = {}
        seen_citations: set[str] = set()

        try:
            async with c.stream("POST", "/chat/completions", json=body) as resp:
                if resp.status_code >= 400:
                    raw = await resp.aread()
                    _raise_payload(resp.status_code, raw, resp.headers.get("x-request-id", ""))

                yield StreamEvent("phase", phase=PHASE_THINKING)

                async for line in resp.aiter_lines():
                    if not line or line.startswith(":"):
                        continue
                    if not line.startswith("data:"):
                        continue
                    data = line[5:].strip()
                    if data == "[DONE]":
                        break
                    try:
                        chunk = orjson.loads(data)
                    except orjson.JSONDecodeError:
                        continue

                    if chunk.get("usage"):
                        usage = chunk["usage"]

                    err = chunk.get("error")
                    if err:
                        raise AnyModelError(
                            str(err.get("message") or "stream error"),
                            status=int(err.get("status") or 503),
                            code=str(err.get("code") or ""),
                        )

                    for choice in chunk.get("choices") or []:
                        delta = choice.get("delta") or {}

                        # размышления: разные провайдеры называют поле по-разному
                        think = (
                            delta.get("reasoning_content")
                            or delta.get("reasoning")
                            or ((delta.get("thinking") or {}).get("content")
                                if isinstance(delta.get("thinking"), dict) else delta.get("thinking"))
                        )
                        if isinstance(think, str) and think:
                            if not reasoning_started:
                                reasoning_started = True
                                yield StreamEvent("phase", phase=PHASE_REASONING)
                            yield StreamEvent("reasoning", text=think)

                        # признак работающего веб-поиска
                        calls = delta.get("tool_calls") or []
                        if calls and not searching:
                            names = " ".join(
                                str((tc.get("function") or {}).get("name") or tc.get("type") or "")
                                for tc in calls
                            )
                            if "search" in names.lower():
                                searching = True
                                yield StreamEvent("phase", phase=PHASE_SEARCHING)

                        for ann in delta.get("annotations") or []:
                            cit = ann.get("url_citation") or {}
                            url = cit.get("url")
                            if url and url not in seen_citations:
                                seen_citations.add(url)
                                yield StreamEvent(
                                    "citations",
                                    citations=[{"url": url, "title": cit.get("title") or url}],
                                )

                        content = delta.get("content")
                        if isinstance(content, list):  # Anthropic-подобная форма блоков
                            content = "".join(
                                b.get("text", "") for b in content if isinstance(b, dict)
                            )
                        if content:
                            if not text_started:
                                text_started = True
                                yield StreamEvent("phase", phase=PHASE_TYPING)
                            yield StreamEvent("delta", text=content)

        except AnyModelError:
            raise
        except (httpx.ReadTimeout, httpx.ConnectTimeout) as e:
            raise AnyModelError("Модель не ответила вовремя", status=503, code="gateway_timeout") from e
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            raise AnyModelError(f"Сеть недоступна: {e}", status=0) from e

        yield StreamEvent("usage", usage=usage)

    async def complete(
        self, *, model: str, messages: list[dict[str, Any]], max_tokens: int = 256
    ) -> tuple[str, Usage]:
        """Нестримовый вызов — для заголовков чатов и служебных задач."""
        c = await self.client()
        r = await c.post(
            "/chat/completions",
            json={"model": model, "messages": messages, "max_tokens": max_tokens},
        )
        _raise_for_error(r)
        data = r.json()
        text = ((data.get("choices") or [{}])[0].get("message") or {}).get("content") or ""
        u = data.get("usage") or {}
        return text.strip(), Usage(
            prompt_tokens=int(u.get("prompt_tokens") or 0),
            completion_tokens=int(u.get("completion_tokens") or 0),
        )


def _raise_for_error(r: httpx.Response) -> None:
    if r.status_code < 400:
        return
    _raise_payload(r.status_code, r.content, r.headers.get("x-request-id", ""))


def _raise_payload(status: int, raw: bytes, request_id: str) -> None:
    message, code = f"HTTP {status}", ""
    try:
        err = (orjson.loads(raw) or {}).get("error") or {}
        message = str(err.get("message") or message)
        code = str(err.get("code") or "")
        request_id = str(err.get("request_id") or request_id)
    except Exception:  # ответ CDN, не JSON
        snippet = raw[:200].decode("utf-8", "replace")
        if snippet:
            message = snippet
    log.warning("anymodel error %s %s %s", status, code, request_id)
    raise AnyModelError(message, status=status, code=code, request_id=request_id)


anymodel = AnyModelClient()

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import io
import logging
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.models import Attachment

log = logging.getLogger("files")

IMAGE_MIME = {"image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif", "image/heic", "image/heif"}
TEXT_SUFFIX = {
    ".txt", ".md", ".markdown", ".csv", ".json", ".yml", ".yaml", ".xml", ".html", ".htm",
    ".py", ".js", ".ts", ".tsx", ".jsx", ".java", ".kt", ".go", ".rs", ".c", ".h", ".cpp",
    ".cs", ".php", ".rb", ".sh", ".sql", ".ini", ".toml", ".cfg", ".log", ".env",
}
MAX_IMAGE_SIDE = 1536
MAX_DOC_CHARS = 60_000


def _new_id() -> str:
    return uuid.uuid4().hex


def is_image(mime: str, name: str) -> bool:
    if mime in IMAGE_MIME:
        return True
    return Path(name).suffix.lower() in {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".gif"}


async def save_upload(
    session: AsyncSession,
    *,
    user_id: int,
    data: bytes,
    file_name: str,
    mime: str,
) -> Attachment:
    limit = settings.max_upload_mb * 1024 * 1024
    if len(data) > limit:
        raise ValueError(f"Файл больше {settings.max_upload_mb} МБ")
    if not data:
        raise ValueError("Пустой файл")

    att_id = _new_id()
    kind = "image" if is_image(mime, file_name) else "doc"
    sub = settings.uploads_dir / str(user_id)
    sub.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    suffix = Path(file_name).suffix[:12] or (".jpg" if kind == "image" else ".bin")
    path = sub / f"{att_id}{suffix}"
    path.write_bytes(data)

    excerpt: str | None = None
    if kind == "image":
        _ = _load_image_bytes(path)  # ранняя проверка: битый файл не доживёт до модели
    else:
        excerpt = extract_text(path, mime)[:MAX_DOC_CHARS] or None

    att = Attachment(
        id=att_id,
        user_id=user_id,
        kind=kind,
        file_name=file_name[:255],
        mime=mime[:128],
        size=len(data),
        rel_path=str(path.relative_to(settings.data_dir)),
        text_excerpt=excerpt,
    )
    session.add(att)
    await session.flush()
    return att


def abs_path(att: Attachment) -> Path:
    return settings.data_dir / att.rel_path


def _load_image_bytes(path: Path) -> bytes:
    """Приводит картинку к JPEG не больше MAX_IMAGE_SIDE: экономит входные токены."""
    from PIL import Image, ImageOps

    with Image.open(path) as im:
        im = ImageOps.exif_transpose(im)
        if im.mode not in ("RGB", "L"):
            im = im.convert("RGB")
        im.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIDE, MAX_IMAGE_SIDE), Image.LANCZOS)
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        im.save(buf, format="JPEG", quality=85, optimize=True)
        return buf.getvalue()


def image_data_url(att: Attachment) -> str:
    raw = _load_image_bytes(abs_path(att))
    return "data:image/jpeg;base64," + base64.b64encode(raw).decode()


def extract_text(path: Path, mime: str = "") -> str:
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()
    try:
        if suffix == ".pdf" or mime == "application/pdf":
            return _pdf_text(path)
        if suffix in (".docx",) or "wordprocessingml" in mime:
            return _docx_text(path)
        if suffix in TEXT_SUFFIX or mime.startswith("text/"):
            return path.read_bytes().decode("utf-8", "replace")
    except Exception as e:
        log.warning("extract %s failed: %s", path.name, e)
        return ""
    # неизвестный формат: пробуем как текст, иначе пусто
    head = path.read_bytes()[:4096]
    if b"\x00" in head:
        return ""
    return path.read_bytes().decode("utf-8", "replace")


def _pdf_text(path: Path) -> str:
    from pypdf import PdfReader

    reader = PdfReader(str(path))
    parts: list[str] = []
    for i, page in enumerate(reader.pages[:60]):
        parts.append(page.extract_text() or "")
        if sum(len(p) for p in parts) > MAX_DOC_CHARS:
            parts.append(f"\n…документ обрезан после {i + 1} страниц…")
            break
    return "\n\n".join(parts).strip()


def _docx_text(path: Path) -> str:
    import docx

    doc = docx.Document(str(path))
    parts = [p.text for p in doc.paragraphs if p.text.strip()]
    for table in doc.tables:
        for row in table.rows:
            parts.append(" | ".join(c.text.strip() for c in row.cells))
    return "\n".join(parts).strip()

from __future__ import annotations

import logging

import httpx

from app.core.config import settings

log = logging.getLogger("notify")
API = "https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/{method}"


async def send_message(chat_id: int, text: str, *, parse_mode: str = "HTML",
                       reply_markup: dict | None = None) -> bool:
    if not settings.bot_token:
        return False
    body: dict = {
        "chat_id": chat_id,
        "text": text[:4096],
        "parse_mode": parse_mode,
        "disable_web_page_preview": True,
    }
    if reply_markup:
        body["reply_markup"] = reply_markup
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=20) as c:
            r = await c.post(API.format(token=settings.bot_token, method="sendMessage"), json=body)
        if r.status_code >= 400:
            log.warning("sendMessage %s: %s", r.status_code, r.text[:200])
            return False
        return True
    except httpx.HTTPError as e:
        log.warning("sendMessage failed: %s", e)
        return False


async def notify_payment(user_id: int, days: int) -> None:
    await send_message(
        user_id,
        f"<b>Оплата прошла</b>\nPro активирован на {days} дней. "
        f"Начислено 1 000 энергии — все модели открыты.",
        reply_markup={
            "inline_keyboard": [[{"text": "Открыть приложение",
                                  "web_app": {"url": settings.webapp_url}}]]
        },
    )

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, AsyncIterator

from sqlalchemy import delete, select

from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.db import session_scope
from app.core.models import Chat, Message, User, utcnow
from app.services import prompt as prompt_svc
from app.services.anymodel import AnyModelError, StreamEvent, anymodel

log = logging.getLogger("pipeline")

PHASE_LABELS = {
    "connecting": "подключаюсь",
    "thinking": "думает",
    "reasoning": "рассуждает",
    "searching": "поиск в интернете",
    "vision": "просматривает фото",
    "typing": "пишет",
    "done": "готово",
}


class GenerationDenied(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, code: str = "denied"):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(slots=True)
class Result:
    text: str = ""
    reasoning: str = ""
    citations: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    tokens_in: int = 0
    tokens_out: int = 0
    searches: int = 0
    cost: int = 0
    energy_left: int = 0
    aborted: bool = False


async def run(
    *,
    user_id: int,
    chat_id: int,
    text: str = "",
    attachments: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    source: str = "web",
    model_path: str | None = None,
    regenerate: bool = False,
) -> AsyncIterator[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Генератор событий SSE. При regenerate=True новое сообщение пользователя
    не добавляется: удаляются ответы после последнего вопроса и он идёт в модель снова.
    """
    attachments = attachments or []

    # ── подготовка ──────────────────────────────────────────
    async with session_scope() as s:
        user = await s.get(User, user_id)
        if user is None:
            yield _err("Пользователь не найден", "no_user")
            return
        if user.is_banned:
            yield _err(user.ban_reason or "Доступ заблокирован", "banned")
            return
        energy_svc.ensure_plan_state(user)

        chat = await s.get(Chat, chat_id)
        if chat is None or chat.user_id != user_id or chat.is_deleted:
            yield _err("Чат не найден", "no_chat")
            return

        plan = catalog.plan(user.plan if user.is_pro else "free")
        model = catalog.get(model_path or chat.model or user.current_model) \
            or catalog.default_model(plan.code)
        if not model.allowed_for(plan.code):
            yield _err(f"Модель «{model.name}» доступна в Pro", "need_pro")
            return
        if not energy_svc.can_afford(user, model):
            yield _err("Закончилась энергия", "no_energy")
            return

        if not regenerate:
            if attachments and plan.max_attachments <= 0:
                yield _err("Вложения доступны в Pro", "need_pro")
                return
            if len(attachments) > max(plan.max_attachments, 0):
                yield _err(f"Не больше {plan.max_attachments} вложений за раз", "too_many")
                return

        chat.model = model.path
        user.current_model = model.path
        chat.updated_at = utcnow()

        rows = await s.execute(
            select(Message).where(Message.chat_id == chat.id).order_by(Message.id)
        )
        history = list(rows.scalars())

        if regenerate:
            last_user = next((m for m in reversed(history) if m.role == "user"), None)
            if last_user is None:
                yield _err("Нечего перегенерировать", "no_question")
                return
            tail = [m.id for m in history if m.id > last_user.id]
            if tail:
                await s.execute(delete(Message).where(Message.id.in_(tail)))
                history = [m for m in history if m.id <= last_user.id]
        else:
            question = Message(
                chat_id=chat.id, role="user", content=text or "",
                attachments=attachments or None, model=model.path,
            )
            s.add(question)
            await s.flush()
            history.append(question)

        new_title = None
        if not regenerate and chat.title in ("Новый чат", "", None):
            new_title = prompt_svc.title_from_text(
                text or (attachments[0]["name"] if attachments else "")
            )
            chat.title = new_title

        payload, has_images = await prompt_svc.build_payload(
            s, history=history, model=model, context_messages=plan.context_messages
        )
        plan_code, model_path_final = plan.code, model.path
        web_search = model.web_search and plan.web_search
        effort = "medium" if model.reasoning else None

    yield {"type": "meta", "model": model_path_final, "title": new_title}

    # ── стрим ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    res = Result()
    chunks: list[str] = []
    think: list[str] = []
    failed: AnyModelError | None = None

    try:
        stream = anymodel.stream_chat(
            model=model_path_final,
            messages=payload,
            web_search=web_search,
            reasoning_effort=effort,
            has_images=has_images,
        )
        async for ev in stream:
            out = _translate(ev)
            if ev.type == "delta":
                chunks.append(ev.text)
            elif ev.type == "reasoning":
                think.append(ev.text)
            elif ev.type == "citations":
                res.citations.extend(ev.citations)
            elif ev.type == "usage":
                res.tokens_in = int(ev.usage.get("prompt_tokens") or 0)
                res.tokens_out = int(ev.usage.get("completion_tokens") or 0)
                res.searches = int(ev.usage.get("web_search_requests") or 0)
            if out:
                yield out
    except AnyModelError as e:
        failed = e
        log.warning("generation failed uid=%s model=%s: %s", user_id, model_path_final, e)
    except (GeneratorExit, KeyboardInterrupt):
        raise
    except BaseException as e:
        res.aborted = True
        log.info("generation aborted uid=%s: %r", user_id, e)
        await _finalize(user_id, chat_id, model_path_final, chunks, think, res, source, plan_code)
        raise

    res.text = "".join(chunks)
    res.reasoning = "".join(think)

    if failed is not None and not res.text:
        async with session_scope() as s:
            user = await s.get(User, user_id)
            if user:
                res.energy_left = energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens)
        yield _err(failed.user_text, failed.code or "upstream")
        return

    msg_id, cost, left = await _finalize(
        user_id, chat_id, model_path_final, chunks, think, res, source, plan_code
    )
    yield {
        "type": "done",
        "message_id": msg_id,
        "cost": cost,
        "energy": left,
        "tokens_in": res.tokens_in,
        "tokens_out": res.tokens_out,
        "searches": res.searches,
        "citations": res.citations,
        "title": new_title,
        "truncated": bool(failed),
    }


async def _finalize(
    user_id: int,
    chat_id: int,
    model_path: str,
    chunks: list[str],
    think: list[str],
    res: Result,
    source: str,
    plan_code: str,
) -> tuple[int | None, int, int]:
    text = "".join(chunks)
    reasoning = "".join(think)
    model = catalog.get(model_path) or catalog.default_model(plan_code)

    if not res.tokens_out and text:
        res.tokens_out = max(1, len(text) // 3)
        res.tokens_in = res.tokens_in or max(1, sum(len(c) for c in chunks) // 12)

    async with session_scope() as s:
        user = await s.get(User, user_id)
        chat = await s.get(Chat, chat_id)
        if user is None or chat is None:
            return None, 0, 0

        if text or reasoning:
            cost = await energy_svc.charge(
                s, user, model, res.tokens_in, res.tokens_out,
                searches=res.searches, source=source, ok=not res.aborted,
            )
            msg = Message(
                chat_id=chat_id, role="assistant", content=text,
                reasoning=reasoning or None, model=model_path,
                tokens_in=res.tokens_in, tokens_out=res.tokens_out, base_cost=cost,
            )
            s.add(msg)
            chat.updated_at = utcnow()
            await s.flush()
            return msg.id, cost, energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens)

        return None, 0, energy_svc.energy_display(user.energy_tokens)


def _translate(ev: StreamEvent) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if ev.type == "phase":
        return {"type": "phase", "phase": ev.phase, "label": PHASE_LABELS.get(ev.phase, "работает")}
    if ev.type == "delta":
        return {"type": "delta", "text": ev.text}
    if ev.type == "reasoning":
        return {"type": "reasoning", "text": ev.text}
    if ev.type == "citations":
        return {"type": "citations", "items": ev.citations}
    return None


def _err(message: str, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"type": "error", "message": message, "code": code}

FILE: app/services/platega.py

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import uuid
from typing import Any

import httpx
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core.catalog import catalog
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.models import Payment, User, utcnow

log = logging.getLogger("platega")

# Статусы транзакции Platega
CONFIRMED = "CONFIRMED"
CANCELED = "CANCELED"
PENDING = "PENDING"
TERMINAL_FAIL = {"CANCELED", "CHARGEBACKED", "EXPIRED", "FAILED"}


class PlategaError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _headers() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        "X-MerchantId": settings.platega_merchant_id,
        "X-Secret": settings.platega_secret,
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    }


def configured() -> bool:
    return bool(settings.platega_merchant_id and settings.platega_secret)


async def create_payment(
    session: AsyncSession, user: User, *, source: str = "web"
) -> Payment:
    """Создаёт транзакцию в Platega и возвращает запись с pay_url."""
    pro = catalog.pro()
    amount = float(pro.price_rub or 150)

    payment = Payment(
        user_id=user.id, amount=amount, currency="RUB",
        status=PENDING, plan="pro", days=pro.days or 30, source=source,
    )
    session.add(payment)
    await session.flush()

    if not configured():
        raise PlategaError("Платежи не настроены: заполните PLATEGA_MERCHANT_ID и PLATEGA_SECRET")

    ref = f"{user.id}-{payment.id}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    body: dict[str, Any] = {
        "paymentMethod": settings.platega_payment_method,
        "amount": amount,
        "currency": "RUB",
        "description": f"Подписка {pro.title} на {pro.days or 30} дней",
        "return": f"{settings.webapp_url.rstrip('/')}/pay/ok?p={payment.id}",
        "failedUrl": f"{settings.webapp_url.rstrip('/')}/pay/fail?p={payment.id}",
        "payload": ref,
        # metadata обязательна для /transaction/process
        "metadata": {"user_id": str(user.id), "payment_id": str(payment.id), "ref": ref},
    }

    url = settings.platega_base_url.rstrip("/") + "/transaction/process"
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30) as c:
        r = await c.post(url, json=body, headers=_headers())
    if r.status_code >= 400:
        log.error("platega create failed %s %s", r.status_code, r.text[:400])
        raise PlategaError(f"Platega вернула {r.status_code}")

    data = r.json()
    payment.tx_id = str(data.get("transactionId") or data.get("id") or "") or None
    payment.pay_url = data.get("redirect") or data.get("url") or data.get("paymentUrl")
    payment.raw = data
    await session.flush()

    if not payment.pay_url:
        raise PlategaError("Platega не вернула ссылку на оплату")
    return payment


async def fetch_status(tx_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    url = settings.platega_base_url.rstrip("/") + f"/transaction/{tx_id}"
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30) as c:
        r = await c.get(url, headers=_headers())
    if r.status_code >= 400:
        raise PlategaError(f"status {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}")
    return r.json()


async def apply_callback(
    session: AsyncSession, data: dict[str, Any]
) -> tuple[Payment | None, bool]:
    """
    Обрабатывает колбэк. Колбэку не доверяем: статус перепроверяется в API.
    Возвращает (платёж, выдана ли подписка сейчас).
    """
    tx_id = str(data.get("id") or data.get("transactionId") or "")
    payload = str(data.get("payload") or "")
    status = str(data.get("status") or "").upper()

    payment = None
    if tx_id:
        payment = (
            await session.execute(select(Payment).where(Payment.tx_id == tx_id))
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
    if payment is None and payload:
        parts = payload.split("-")
        if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[1].isdigit():
            payment = await session.get(Payment, int(parts[1]))
    if payment is None:
        log.warning("callback for unknown tx %s payload=%s", tx_id, payload)
        return None, False

    if payment.applied:
        return payment, False  # идемпотентность: повторный колбэк не выдаёт Pro дважды

    if status == CONFIRMED and payment.tx_id:
        try:
            remote = await fetch_status(payment.tx_id)
            status = str(remote.get("status") or status).upper()
            payment.raw = {"callback": data, "verified": remote}
        except PlategaError as e:
            log.error("verification failed for %s: %s", payment.tx_id, e)
            return payment, False  # Platega повторит колбэк

    payment.status = status
    if status != CONFIRMED:
        await session.flush()
        return payment, False

    user = await session.get(User, payment.user_id)
    if user is None:
        return payment, False

    energy_svc.grant_pro(user, payment.days)
    user.paid_total = (user.paid_total or 0) + int(payment.amount)
    payment.applied = True
    payment.paid_at = utcnow()
    await session.flush()
    log.info("pro granted uid=%s payment=%s", user.id, payment.id)
    return payment, True


def callback_authorized(headers: dict[str, str]) -> bool:
    """
    Platega может подписывать колбэк теми же заголовками мерчанта.
    Если проверка включена, но заголовков нет, пропускаем: статус всё равно
    перепроверяется запросом к API, а жёсткий отказ потеряет уведомление.
    """
    if not settings.platega_callback_secret_check:
        return True
    got = headers.get("x-secret") or headers.get("X-Secret")
    if not got:
        return True
    return got == settings.platega_secret

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core import energy as energy_svc
from app.core.models import Promo, PromoUse, User, utcnow


class PromoError(Exception):
    pass


async def redeem(session: AsyncSession, user: User, code: str) -> str:
    code = (code or "").strip().upper()
    if not code:
        raise PromoError("Введите промокод")

    promo = (
        await session.execute(select(Promo).where(Promo.code == code))
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    if promo is None or not promo.is_active:
        raise PromoError("Промокод не найден")
    if promo.expires_at and promo.expires_at <= utcnow():
        raise PromoError("Промокод истёк")
    if promo.max_uses and promo.used >= promo.max_uses:
        raise PromoError("Промокод уже использован")

    already = (
        await session.execute(
            select(PromoUse).where(PromoUse.promo_id == promo.id, PromoUse.user_id == user.id)
        )
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    if already:
        raise PromoError("Вы уже активировали этот промокод")

    if promo.kind == "energy":
        energy_svc.add_energy(user, promo.energy)
        message = f"Начислено {promo.energy} энергии"
    else:
        energy_svc.grant_pro(user, promo.days)
        message = f"Pro активирован на {promo.days} дней"

    promo.used += 1
    session.add(PromoUse(promo_id=promo.id, user_id=user.id))
    await session.flush()
    return message
